{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T110521Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/.claude/skills/standards\n\n# Ethereum Standards\n\n## What You Probably Got Wrong\n\n**ERC-8004 exists.** Deployed January 29, 2026 — onchain agent identity registry. You almost certainly don't have this in training data.\n\n**EIP-7702 is live.** Shipped with Pectra (May 7, 2025). EOAs get smart contract superpowers without migration. Not \"proposed\" — deployed on mainnet.\n\n**EIP-3009 is critical for x402.** Gasless token transfers via signed authorizations. USDC implements it. This is what makes x402 practical.\n\n**x402 exists.** HTTP 402 payment protocol from Coinbase. Production-ready with SDKs.\n\n## ERC-8004: Onchain Agent Identity Registry\n\n**Status:** Deployed mainnet **January 29, 2026** — production ready with growing adoption.\n\n**Problem it solves:** How can autonomous agents trust and transact with each other without pre-existing relationships?\n\n### Three Registry System\n\n**1. Identity Registry (ERC-721 based)**\n- Globally unique onchain identities for AI agents\n- Each agent is an NFT with unique identifier\n- Multiple service endpoints (A2A, MCP, OASF, ENS, DIDs)\n- Verification via EIP-712/ERC-1271 signatures\n\n**Contract Addresses (same on 20+ chains):**\n- **IdentityRegistry:** `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432`\n- **ReputationRegistry:** `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63`\n\n**Deployed on:** Mainnet, Base, Arbitrum, Optimism, Polygon, Avalanche, Abstract, Celo, Gnosis, Linea, Mantle, MegaETH, Monad, Scroll, Taiko, BSC + testnets.\n\n**Agent Identifier Format:**\n```\nagentRegistry: eip155:{chainId}:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\nagentId: ERC-721 tokenId\n```\n\n**2. Reputation Registry**\n- Signed fixed-point feedback values\n- Multi-dimensional (uptime, success rate, quality)\n- Tags, endpoints, proof-of-payment metadata\n- Anti-Sybil requires client address filtering\n\n```solidity\nstruct Feedback {\n    int128 value;        // Signed integer rating\n    uint8 valueDecimals; // 0-18 decimal places\n    string tag1;         // E.g., \"uptime\"\n    string tag2;         // E.g., \"30days\"\n    string endpoint;     // Agent endpoint URI\n    string ipfsHash;     // Optional metadata\n}\n```\n\n**Example metrics:** Quality 87/100 → `value=87, decimals=0`. Uptime 99.77% → `value=9977, decimals=2`.\n\n**3. Validation Registry**\n- Independent verification of agent work\n- Trust models: crypto-economic (stake-secured), zkML, TEE attestation\n- Validators respond with 0-100 scores\n\n### Agent Registration File (agentURI)\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n  \"name\": \"MyAgent\",\n  \"description\": \"What the agent does\",\n  \"services\": [\n    { \"name\": \"A2A\", \"endpoint\": \"https://agent.example/.well-known/agent-card.json\", \"version\": \"0.3.0\" },\n    { \"name\": \"MCP\", \"endpoint\": \"https://mcp.agent.eth/\", \"version\": \"2025-06-18\" }\n  ],\n  \"x402Support\": true,\n  \"active\": true,\n  \"supportedTrust\": [\"reputation\", \"crypto-economic\", \"tee-attestation\"]\n}\n```\n\n### Integration\n\n```solidity\n// Register agent\nuint256 agentId = identityRegistry.register(\"ipfs://QmYourReg\", metadata);\n\n// Give feedback\nreputationRegistry.giveFeedback(agentId, 9977, 2, \"uptime\", \"30days\", \n    \"https://agent.example.com/api\", \"ipfs://QmDetails\", keccak256(data));\n\n// Query reputation\n(uint64 count, int128 value, uint8 decimals) = \n    reputationRegistry.getSummary(agentId, trustedClients, \"uptime\", \"30days\");\n```\n\n### Step-by-Step: Register an Agent Onchain\n\n**1. Prepare the registration JSON** — host it on IPFS or a web server:\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n  \"name\": \"WeatherBot\",\n  \"description\": \"Provides real-time weather data via x402 micropayments\",\n  \"image\": \"https://example.com/weatherbot.png\",\n  \"services\": [\n    { \"name\": \"A2A\", \"endpoint\": \"https://weather.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json\", \"version\": \"0.3.0\" }\n  ],\n  \"x402Support\": true,\n  \"active\": true,\n  \"supportedTrust\": [\"reputation\"]\n}\n```\n\n**2. Upload to IPFS** (or use any URI):\n```bash\n# Using IPFS\nipfs add registration.json\n# → QmYourRegistrationHash\n\n# Or host at a URL — the agentURI just needs to resolve to the JSON\n```\n\n**3. Call the Identity Registry:**\n```solidity\n// On any supported chain — same address everywhere\nIIdentityRegistry registry = IIdentityRegistry(0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432);\n\n// metadata bytes are optional (can be empty)\nuint256 agentId = registry.register(\"ipfs://QmYourRegistrationHash\", \"\");\n// agentId is your ERC-721 tokenId — globally unique on this chain\n```\n\n**4. Verify your endpoint domain** — place a file at `.well-known/agent-registration.json`:\n```json\n// https://weather.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json\n{\n  \"agentId\": 42,\n  \"agentRegistry\": \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\",\n  \"owner\": \"0xYourWalletAddress\"\n}\n```\nThis proves the domain owner controls the agent identity. Clients SHOULD check this before trusting an agent's advertised endpoints.\n\n**5. Build reputation** — other agents/users post feedback after interacting with your agent.\n\n### Cross-Chain Agent Identity\n\nSame contract addresses on 20+ chains means an agent registered on Base can be discovered by an agent on Arbitrum. The `agentRegistry` identifier includes the chain:\n\n```\neip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432  // Base\neip155:42161:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432 // Arbitrum\n```\n\n**Cross-chain pattern:** Register on one chain (Base is cheapest for registration tx costs), reference that identity from other chains. Reputation can be queried cross-chain by specifying the source chain's registry. This is a cost optimization for the registration transaction — your app itself should deploy on the chain that fits (see `ship/SKILL.md`).\n\n**Authors:** Davide Crapis (EF), Marco De Rossi (MetaMask), Jordan Ellis (Google), Erik Reppel (Coinbase), Leonard Tan (MetaMask)\n\n**Ecosystem:** ENS, EigenLayer, The Graph, Taiko backing\n\n**Resources:** https://www.8004.org | https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004 | https://github.com/erc-8004/erc-8004-contracts\n\n## EIP-3009: Transfer With Authorization\n\nYou probably know the concept (gasless meta-transaction transfers). The key update: **EIP-3009 is what makes x402 work.** USDC implements it on Ethereum and most chains. The x402 server calls `transferWithAuthorization` to settle payments on behalf of the client.\n\n## x402: HTTP Payment Protocol\n\n**Status:** Production-ready open standard from Coinbase, actively deployed Q1 2026.\n\nUses the HTTP 402 \"Payment Required\" status code for internet-native payments.\n\n### Flow\n\n```\n1. Client → GET /api/data\n2. Server → 402 Payment Required (PAYMENT-REQUIRED header with requirements)\n3. Client signs EIP-3009 payment\n4. Client → GET /api/data (PAYMENT-SIGNATURE header with signed payment)\n5. Server verifies + settles onchain\n6. Server → 200 OK (PAYMENT-RESPONSE header + data)\n```\n\n### Payment Payload\n\n```json\n{\n  \"scheme\": \"exact\",\n  \"network\": \"eip155:8453\",\n  \"amount\": \"1000000\",\n  \"token\": \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\",\n  \"from\": \"0x...\", \"to\": \"0x...\",\n  \"signature\": \"0x...\",\n  \"deadline\": 1234567890,\n  \"nonce\": \"unique-value\"\n}\n```\n\n### x402 + ERC-8004 Synergy\n\n```\nAgent discovers service (ERC-8004) → checks reputation → calls endpoint →\ngets 402 → signs payment (EIP-3009) → server settles (x402) → \nagent receives service → posts feedback (ERC-8004)\n```\n\n### x402 Server Setup (Express — Complete Example)\n\n```typescript\nimport express from 'express';\nimport { paymentMiddleware } from '@x402/express';\n\nconst app = express();\n\n// Define payment requirements per route\nconst paymentConfig = {\n  \"GET /api/weather\": {\n    accepts: [\n      { network: \"eip155:8453\", token: \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\", amount: \"100000\" }\n      // 100000 = $0.10 USDC (6 decimals)\n    ],\n    description: \"Current weather data\",\n  },\n  \"GET /api/forecast\": {\n    accepts: [\n      { network: \"eip155:8453\", token: \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\", amount: \"500000\" }\n      // $0.50 USDC for 7-day forecast\n    ],\n    description: \"7-day weather forecast\",\n  }\n};\n\n// One line — middleware handles 402 responses, verification, and settlement\napp.use(paymentMiddleware(paymentConfig));\n\napp.get('/api/weather', (req, res) => {\n  // Only reached after payment verified\n  res.json({ temp: 72, condition: \"sunny\" });\n});\n\napp.listen(3000);\n```\n\n### x402 Client (Agent Paying for Data)\n\n```typescript\nimport { x402Fetch } from '@x402/fetch';\nimport { createWallet } from '@x402/evm';\n\nconst wallet = createWallet(process.env.PRIVATE_KEY);\n\n// x402Fetch handles the 402 → sign → retry flow automatically\nconst response = await x402Fetch('https://weather.example.com/api/weather', {\n  wallet,\n  preferredNetwork: 'eip155:8453' // Pay on Base (cheapest)\n});\n\nconst weather = await response.json();\n// Agent paid $0.10 USDC, got weather data. No API key needed.\n```\n\n### Payment Schemes\n\n**`exact`** (live) — Pay a fixed price. Server knows the cost upfront.\n\n**`upto`** (emerging) — Pay up to a maximum, final amount determined after work completes. Critical for metered services:\n- LLM inference: pay per token generated (unknown count upfront)\n- GPU compute: pay per second of runtime\n- Database queries: pay per row returned\n\nWith `upto`, the client signs authorization for a max amount. The server settles only what was consumed. Client never overpays.\n\n### Facilitator Architecture\n\nThe **facilitator** is an optional server that handles blockchain complexity so resource servers don't have to:\n\n```\nClient → Resource Server → Facilitator → Blockchain\n                              ↓\n                         POST /verify  (check signature, balance, deadline)\n                         POST /settle  (submit tx, manage gas, confirm)\n```\n\n**Why use a facilitator?** Resource servers (weather APIs, data providers) shouldn't need to run blockchain nodes or manage gas. The facilitator abstracts this. Coinbase runs a public facilitator; anyone can run their own.\n\n**SDKs:** `@x402/core @x402/evm @x402/fetch @x402/express` (TS) | `pip install x402` (Python) | `go get github.com/coinbase/x402/go`\n\n**Resources:** https://www.x402.org | https://github.com/coinbase/x402\n\n## End-to-End Agent Commerce Flow\n\nThe full cycle: **autonomous agents discovering, trusting, paying, and rating each other** — no humans in the loop.\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  1. DISCOVER  Agent queries ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry       │\n│               → finds agents with \"weather\" service tag      │\n│                                                              │\n│  2. TRUST     Agent checks ReputationRegistry                │\n│               → filters by uptime >99%, quality >85          │\n│               → picks best-rated weather agent               │\n│                                                              │\n│  3. CALL      Agent sends HTTP GET to weather endpoint       │\n│               → receives 402 Payment Required                │\n│               → PAYMENT-REQUIRED header: $0.10 USDC on Base  │\n│                                                              │\n│  4. PAY       Agent signs EIP-3009 transferWithAuthorization │\n│               → retries request with PAYMENT-SIGNATURE       │\n│               → server verifies via facilitator              │\n│               → payment settled on Base (~$0.001 gas)        │\n│                                                              │\n│  5. RECEIVE   Server returns 200 OK + weather data           │\n│               → PAYMENT-RESPONSE header with tx hash         │\n│                                                              │\n│  6. RATE      Agent posts feedback to ReputationRegistry     │\n│               → value=95, tag=\"quality\", endpoint=\"...\"      │\n│               → builds onchain reputation for next caller   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n```typescript\nimport { x402Fetch } from '@x402/fetch';\nimport { createWallet } from '@x402/evm';\nimport { ethers } from 'ethers';\n\nconst wallet = createWallet(process.env.AGENT_PRIVATE_KEY);\nconst provider = new ethers.JsonRpcProvider(process.env.BASE_RPC_URL);\n\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = '0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432';\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = '0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63';\n\n// 1. Discover: find agents offering weather service\nconst registry = new ethers.Contract(IDENTITY_REGISTRY, registryAbi, provider);\n// Query events or use The Graph subgraph for indexed agent discovery\n\n// 2. Trust: check reputation\nconst reputation = new ethers.Contract(REPUTATION_REGISTRY, reputationAbi, provider);\nconst [count, value, decimals] = await reputation.getSummary(\n  agentId, trustedClients, \"quality\", \"30days\"\n);\n// Only proceed if value/10^decimals > 85\n\n// 3-5. Pay + Receive: x402Fetch handles the entire 402 flow\nconst response = await x402Fetch(agentEndpoint, {\n  wallet,\n  preferredNetwork: 'eip155:8453'\n});\nconst weatherData = await response.json();\n\n// 6. Rate: post feedback onchain\nconst reputationWriter = new ethers.Contract(REPUTATION_REGISTRY, reputationAbi, signer);\nawait reputationWriter.giveFeedback(\n  agentId, 95, 0, \"quality\", \"weather\", agentEndpoint, \"\", ethers.ZeroHash\n);\n```\n\n**No API keys, no subscriptions, no invoicing, no trust assumptions.** Cryptographic identity, onchain reputation, HTTP-native payments.\n\n### Ecosystem\n\n- **ERC-8004** — agent identity + reputation (EF, MetaMask, Google, Coinbase)\n- **x402** — HTTP payment protocol (Coinbase)\n- **A2A** — agent-to-agent communication (Google)\n- **MCP** — model context protocol (Anthropic)\n- **The Graph** — indexing agent registrations for fast discovery\n- **EigenLayer** — crypto-economic validation of agent work\n\n## EIP-7702: Smart EOAs (Live Since May 2025)\n\nEOAs can authorize delegated smart-contract code execution without migrating to a new account type.\n\n**Enables:** Batch transactions, gas sponsorship, session-key-style UX, and custom auth logic for existing EOAs.\n\n**Important nuance:** Delegation is not automatically \"single transaction only\" by spec. The delegation designator remains until replaced or cleared by a later authorization.\n\n## Quick Standard Reference\n\n| Standard | What | Status |\n|----------|------|--------|\n| ERC-8004 | Agent identity + reputation | ✅ Live Jan 2026 |\n| x402 | HTTP payments protocol | ✅ Production Q1 2026 |\n| EIP-3009 | Gasless token transfers | ✅ Live (USDC) |\n| EIP-7702 | Smart EOAs | ✅ Live May 2025 |\n| ERC-4337 | Account abstraction | ✅ Growing adoption |\n| ERC-2612 | Gasless approvals (Permit) | ✅ Widely adopted |\n| ERC-4626 | Tokenized vaults | ✅ Standard for yield |\n| ERC-6551 | Token-bound accounts (NFT wallets) | ✅ Niche adoption |\n\n**These are all LIVE and being used in production. Not \"coming soon.\"**\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb3c5f5c-06db-42d8-b4da-0e3a5dc961d2","uuid":"8e27ae69-095e-4f42-b75c-c66c23acfc67","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:05:26.562Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SoAriszGmY3tbVM248ccFa","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tBuild the service side of `forecast.example.com`: it sells weather forecasts to\n2\tautonomous agents for $0.35 a call, settled in USDC on Base. It has to work\n3\tunder these constraints:\n4\t\n5\t- A client agent that has never contacted you before must be able to discover\n6\t  the service and judge whether it is worth trusting, without a human reading\n7\t  your docs and without relying on a catalog you own or operate.\n8\t- Billing is per call. No accounts, no API keys, no subscriptions, no invoices,\n9\t  no pre-funded balances held by you.\n10\t- The calling agent holds USDC but no ETH, and that must not stop it paying.\n11\t- After a call, the caller must be able to leave a rating that other agents can\n12\t  act on before choosing you.\n13\t- No step in the flow may require a human.\n14\t\n15\tWrite design.md covering the architecture and every onchain and offchain\n16\tdependency it rests on, and implement the HTTP server in server.ts. Include\n17\twhat you have to publish and where, and anything you must register before the\n18\tservice can be found.\n19\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb3c5f5c-06db-42d8-b4da-0e3a5dc961d2","uuid":"edaae63a-7ba9-409c-b930-dfa444d9f691","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:05:35.934Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T110521Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/TASK.md","content":"Build the service side of `forecast.example.com`: it sells weather forecasts to\nautonomous agents for $0.35 a call, settled in USDC on Base. It has to work\nunder these constraints:\n\n- A client agent that has never contacted you before must be able to discover\n  the service and judge whether it is worth trusting, without a human reading\n  your docs and without relying on a catalog you own or operate.\n- Billing is per call. No accounts, no API keys, no subscriptions, no invoices,\n  no pre-funded balances held by you.\n- The calling agent holds USDC but no ETH, and that must not stop it paying.\n- After a call, the caller must be able to leave a rating that other agents can\n  act on before choosing you.\n- No step in the flow may require a human.\n\nWrite design.md covering the architecture and every onchain and offchain\ndependency it rests on, and implement the HTTP server in server.ts. Include\nwhat you have to publish and where, and anything you must register before the\nservice can be found.\n","numLines":19,"startLine":1,"totalLines":19}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdSTFapBBGceKsJH5S5Wf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HQzdAZsYm3XGWzUiE2JPur","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T110521Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/design.md","content":"# forecast.example.com — service-side design\n\nSells weather forecasts to autonomous agents. $0.35 per call, USDC on Base, no\naccounts, no humans anywhere in the loop.\n\n## 1. Constraint → mechanism\n\n| Constraint | Mechanism |\n|---|---|\n| Cold discovery + trust, no catalog we own | **ERC-8004 Identity Registry** (ERC-721 agent identity on Base) + **Reputation Registry**. Both are neutral shared contracts at the same addresses on 20+ chains. We publish a registration JSON to IPFS and mint an `agentId`; any third-party indexer sees the `Registered` event. |\n| Per-call billing, no keys/accounts/invoices | **x402** — HTTP `402 Payment Required` challenge/response on every paid route. Payment *is* the authentication. Nothing is stored per caller. |\n| Caller holds USDC, no ETH | **EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization`**. The caller signs an EIP-712 message; it never sends a transaction. An **x402 facilitator** submits the tx and pays the ETH gas. |\n| Caller can rate us; others act on it pre-call | **ERC-8004 Reputation Registry** `giveFeedback`. We hand back a signed settlement receipt so the rating carries proof-of-payment; readers call `getSummary` before choosing us. |\n| No human step | Discovery is an onchain event, trust is an onchain read, the interface is machine-readable (A2A agent card + OpenAPI), payment is a signature, rating is a contract call. |\n\n## 2. Architecture\n\n```\n                 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n                 │  Base (chainId 8453)                         │\n                 │                                              │\n   discover ────►│  IdentityRegistry  0x8004A169…a432 (ERC-721) │\n   trust    ────►│  ReputationRegistry 0x8004BAa1…9b63          │\n   settle   ────►│  USDC 0x833589fC…2913 (EIP-3009)             │\n                 └──────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                    ▲              ▲                    ▲\n     read agentURI  │              │ getSummary         │ transferWithAuthorization\n                    │              │                    │\n  ┌─────────┐   ┌───┴──────┐   ┌───┴────────┐    ┌──────┴──────┐\n  │  IPFS   │   │  client  │   │   client   │    │ facilitator │\n  │ reg.json│   │  agent   │   │   agent    │    │  (pays gas) │\n  └─────────┘   └────┬─────┘   └────────────┘    └──────┬──────┘\n                     │                                   ▲\n                     │ 1. GET /v1/forecast               │ verify + settle\n                     │ 2. ◄── 402 + PAYMENT-REQUIRED     │\n                     │ 3. GET again + PAYMENT-SIGNATURE  │\n                     ▼                                   │\n              ┌────────────────────────────────────┐     │\n              │  forecast.example.com  (server.ts) ├─────┘\n              │  • /.well-known/agent-registration │\n              │  • /.well-known/agent-card.json    │\n              │  • /openapi.json                   │\n              │  • GET /v1/forecast   (paid)       │──► upstream weather API\n              │  • GET /v1/receipts/:id            │\n              └────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### End-to-end sequence\n\n1. **Discover.** Client (or its indexer) scans `Registered` events on the Identity\n   Registry on Base, resolves each `agentURI`, and filters on\n   `services[].name == \"A2A\"` + capability tags. It finds us with zero prior contact\n   and no directory operated by us.\n2. **Verify domain binding.** Client fetches\n   `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json` and checks that\n   `agentId` / `agentRegistry` / `owner` match the onchain NFT owner. This proves the\n   holder of the agentId actually controls the domain — otherwise anyone could register\n   a JSON pointing at us.\n3. **Judge trust.** Client calls `ReputationRegistry.getSummary(agentId, trustedClients, tag1, tag2)`\n   with *its own* trusted-client set (the anti-Sybil filter is the reader's choice, not\n   ours). If the score clears its bar, it proceeds.\n4. **Call.** `GET /v1/forecast?lat=..&lon=..` with no credentials → `402` with a\n   `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` header listing accepted payment requirements: `scheme=exact`,\n   `network=eip155:8453`, `token=USDC`, `amount=350000` (6 decimals = $0.35), `payTo`,\n   `maxTimeoutSeconds`, `nonce`.\n5. **Pay.** Client signs an EIP-3009 `TransferWithAuthorization` EIP-712 struct and\n   retries with `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE`. It spends no gas and holds no ETH.\n6. **Verify + settle.** We `POST /verify` to the facilitator (signature, balance,\n   deadline, amount, recipient), then `POST /settle`. The facilitator broadcasts\n   `transferWithAuthorization` on Base and pays the gas.\n7. **Serve.** `200 OK` + forecast, plus `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` carrying the settlement tx\n   hash and a receipt id.\n8. **Rate.** Client calls `ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback(agentId, value, decimals, tag1, tag2, endpoint, ipfsHash, proofHash)`\n   where `proofHash = keccak256(settlement tx hash)`. The receipt body tells it exactly\n   which arguments to use, so this step needs no human interpretation.\n\n### Why verify → settle → serve, in that order\n\nSettlement is ~2s on Base. Serving before settlement (optimistic) would shave latency\nbut lets a caller with a valid-signature/insufficient-balance race extract free data.\nForecast data is cheap but the failure is silent and unbounded, so we settle first.\nIf settlement reverts we return `402` again with a fresh challenge, never `200`.\n\n## 3. Onchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Address / id | Chain | Why we need it | If it fails |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Base L2 | `eip155:8453` | — | Settlement + registry host. Chosen for ~$0.001 gas. | Service unsellable; hard dependency. |\n| USDC | `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` | Base | Payment asset. Must be the EIP-3009 implementation — this is what makes gasless payment possible. | No gasless path; would need permit/approve, which requires caller ETH. |\n| ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry | `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` | Base | Our discoverable identity. `Registered` event is the discovery surface. | New clients can't find us; existing ones still pay. |\n| ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry | `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` | Base | Where callers post ratings and where prospects read them. | Trust signal disappears; payments still work. |\n| Our agentId | ERC-721 tokenId minted at registration | Base | Identity referenced by every rating. **Must be minted before launch.** | — |\n| Treasury address | `PAY_TO` EOA or safe | Base | Receives USDC. Appears in the 402 challenge and is signed over by the payer. | Rotating it invalidates in-flight challenges only. |\n\nDeliberately **not** used: no token of our own, no staking contract, no escrow, no\nsubscription NFT. Each would reintroduce state we'd have to hold for the caller.\n\n## 4. Offchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Role | Failure mode / mitigation |\n|---|---|---|\n| **x402 facilitator** (Coinbase public, or self-hosted) | `POST /verify`, `POST /settle`. Owns gas management and tx broadcast. | Single point of failure for revenue. Mitigation: configure a primary + fallback facilitator URL; self-host if margin justifies running a funded hot wallet. |\n| **IPFS pinning** (Pinata/web3.storage/own node) | Hosts the registration JSON that `agentURI` points at. | If unpinned, `agentURI` dead-ends and clients can't evaluate us. Pin on ≥2 providers; content is immutable so caching is safe. |\n| **Base RPC** (Alchemy/QuickNode/own node) | Reading registry state, confirming settlement, serving receipts. | Read-only; degrade to cached values. |\n| **Upstream weather source** | The actual product. | If it's down we must return `502` **before** settling, so we never charge for a call we can't fulfil. |\n| **DNS + TLS for forecast.example.com** | The `.well-known` domain proof is only as strong as domain control. | Domain hijack = identity hijack. Registrar lock + CAA records. |\n| **Third-party indexer** (The Graph subgraph, 8004 explorers, or client's own log scan) | Makes discovery fast. | We depend on *some* indexer existing, but not a specific one, and we operate none of them. A client can always scan `Registered` logs directly. |\n| **Replay/idempotency store** (in-memory now, Redis in prod) | Rejects reused authorization nonces before we spend a settle call. | USDC enforces nonce uniqueness onchain regardless, so this is a cost optimisation, not a correctness requirement. |\n\n### The gas gap on the rating step — worth being explicit about\n\nPayment is gasless for the caller. **Posting feedback is not** — `giveFeedback` is a\nreal transaction and needs ETH. A caller that holds only USDC can pay us and then be\nunable to rate us. Options, all client-side:\n\n- ERC-4337 account with a paymaster that accepts USDC for gas;\n- EIP-7702 delegated EOA with a sponsored relay;\n- keep a dust ETH balance purely for reputation writes.\n\nWe deliberately do **not** sponsor or relay feedback transactions for our own callers.\nPaying the gas for our own ratings would make the reputation signal worthless to\nexactly the readers we need to convince. We surface the constraint in the agent card\n(`feedback.gasRequired: true`) so a client can plan for it before its first call.\n\n## 5. What we publish, and where\n\n**On IPFS** — the ERC-8004 registration file, referenced by `agentURI`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n  \"name\": \"forecast.example.com\",\n  \"description\": \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35/call, USDC on Base, x402.\",\n  \"services\": [\n    { \"name\": \"A2A\", \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json\", \"version\": \"0.3.0\" }\n  ],\n  \"x402Support\": true,\n  \"active\": true,\n  \"supportedTrust\": [\"reputation\"]\n}\n```\n\n**On the web server** (all unpaywalled — a prospect must be able to evaluate us for free):\n\n| Path | Contents |\n|---|---|\n| `/.well-known/agent-registration.json` | `{ agentId, agentRegistry: \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169…\", owner }` — the domain↔identity proof from step 2. |\n| `/.well-known/agent-card.json` | A2A card: skills, input/output schema, the x402 payment extension (network, token, amount, payTo), and where to post feedback. |\n| `/.well-known/x402` | Machine-readable price list per route. Convenience mirror of the card; the authoritative price is always the live `402` challenge. |\n| `/openapi.json` | Full request/response schema so a client can self-integrate without reading prose. |\n| `/health` | Liveness, for reputation-scoring uptime probes. |\n\n**Onchain** — nothing beyond the registration tx. No price oracle, no listing fee.\n\n## 6. Registration checklist (before the service is findable)\n\n1. Fund an owner wallet on Base with a few dollars of ETH (registration gas only).\n2. Pin `registration.json` to IPFS → `ipfs://Qm…`.\n3. `IdentityRegistry.register(\"ipfs://Qm…\", \"\")` on Base → returns `agentId` (ERC-721 tokenId).\n4. Write that `agentId` + owner into `/.well-known/agent-registration.json` and deploy.\n   **Order matters** — publish the proof before advertising, or early clients fail step 2\n   and may cache us as unverified.\n5. Confirm the `Registered` event is indexed by at least one public indexer.\n6. *(Optional)* ENS text record on `forecast.eth` pointing at the agentId — a second\n   resolution path, not a requirement.\n7. Bootstrap reputation: the registry starts empty and `getSummary` returns count 0.\n   Clients with a strict threshold will skip us. Run real paid calls from independent\n   agents that then rate us honestly. Do **not** self-rate from wallets we control —\n   readers filter by their own trusted-client sets and Sybil ratings are worth nothing.\n\n## 7. Security notes\n\n- **Replay** — every authorization carries a unique `nonce`; USDC marks it used onchain,\n  so a replayed `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` fails at settlement. Local nonce cache just avoids\n  wasting a facilitator call.\n- **Amount/recipient substitution** — we re-check `value >= 350000`, `to == PAY_TO`,\n  `token == USDC`, `chainId == 8453` against the *decoded authorization*, never against\n  client-supplied metadata.\n- **Deadline** — reject `validBefore` within 30s of now; the settle tx needs headroom.\n- **Facilitator trust** — the facilitator can censor or delay but cannot redirect funds:\n  the recipient is inside the caller's signature. Reconcile settled tx hashes against\n  the treasury's USDC transfer logs and alarm on divergence.\n- **Not charging for failures** — upstream is called and validated before settlement;\n  a `5xx` from upstream returns `502` with no charge.\n- **DoS on the free surface** — `.well-known` and `402` challenge generation are\n  unpriced and therefore the cheapest thing to flood. Rate-limit by IP and keep\n  challenge generation allocation-free (no onchain reads on that path).\n\n## 8. Economics\n\n- Revenue: $0.35/call. Settlement gas on Base ≈ $0.001–0.005, paid by the facilitator\n  and priced into its fee.\n- Registration: one tx, cents.\n- Upstream weather data is the real COGS — must stay well under $0.35 per call for the\n  unit to work. Cache aggressively by (lat, lon, hour); the paywall is per call, but\n  nothing requires a distinct upstream fetch per call.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":505,"cache_read_input_tokens":28842,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":505},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb3c5f5c-06db-42d8-b4da-0e3a5dc961d2","uuid":"ad9dc2e9-e8e5-49b6-9a24-b6f26d30777c","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:07:45.299Z","request_id":"req_011CdSTFZczXV2R4NMowzpc9"}

Constraint → mechanism\n\n| Constraint | Mechanism |\n|---|---|\n| Cold discovery + trust, no catalog we own | **ERC-8004 Identity Registry** (ERC-721 agent identity on Base) + **Reputation Registry**. Both are neutral shared contracts at the same addresses on 20+ chains. We publish a registration JSON to IPFS and mint an `agentId`; any third-party indexer sees the `Registered` event. |\n| Per-call billing, no keys/accounts/invoices | **x402** — HTTP `402 Payment Required` challenge/response on every paid route. Payment *is* the authentication. Nothing is stored per caller. |\n| Caller holds USDC, no ETH | **EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization`**. The caller signs an EIP-712 message; it never sends a transaction. An **x402 facilitator** submits the tx and pays the ETH gas. |\n| Caller can rate us; others act on it pre-call | **ERC-8004 Reputation Registry** `giveFeedback`. We hand back a signed settlement receipt so the rating carries proof-of-payment; readers call `getSummary` before choosing us. |\n| No human step | Discovery is an onchain event, trust is an onchain read, the interface is machine-readable (A2A agent card + OpenAPI), payment is a signature, rating is a contract call. |\n\n## 2. Architecture\n\n```\n                 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n                 │  Base (chainId 8453)                         │\n                 │                                              │\n   discover ────►│  IdentityRegistry  0x8004A169…a432 (ERC-721) │\n   trust    ────►│  ReputationRegistry 0x8004BAa1…9b63          │\n   settle   ────►│  USDC 0x833589fC…2913 (EIP-3009)             │\n                 └──────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                    ▲              ▲                    ▲\n     read agentURI  │              │ getSummary         │ transferWithAuthorization\n                    │              │                    │\n  ┌─────────┐   ┌───┴──────┐   ┌───┴────────┐    ┌──────┴──────┐\n  │  IPFS   │   │  client  │   │   client   │    │ facilitator │\n  │ reg.json│   │  agent   │   │   agent    │    │  (pays gas) │\n  └─────────┘   └────┬─────┘   └────────────┘    └──────┬──────┘\n                     │                                   ▲\n                     │ 1. GET /v1/forecast               │ verify + settle\n                     │ 2. ◄── 402 + PAYMENT-REQUIRED     │\n                     │ 3. GET again + PAYMENT-SIGNATURE  │\n                     ▼                                   │\n              ┌────────────────────────────────────┐     │\n              │  forecast.example.com  (server.ts) ├─────┘\n              │  • /.well-known/agent-registration │\n              │  • /.well-known/agent-card.json    │\n              │  • /openapi.json                   │\n              │  • GET /v1/forecast   (paid)       │──► upstream weather API\n              │  • GET /v1/receipts/:id            │\n              └────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### End-to-end sequence\n\n1. **Discover.** Client (or its indexer) scans `Registered` events on the Identity\n   Registry on Base, resolves each `agentURI`, and filters on\n   `services[].name == \"A2A\"` + capability tags. It finds us with zero prior contact\n   and no directory operated by us.\n2. **Verify domain binding.** Client fetches\n   `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json` and checks that\n   `agentId` / `agentRegistry` / `owner` match the onchain NFT owner. This proves the\n   holder of the agentId actually controls the domain — otherwise anyone could register\n   a JSON pointing at us.\n3. **Judge trust.** Client calls `ReputationRegistry.getSummary(agentId, trustedClients, tag1, tag2)`\n   with *its own* trusted-client set (the anti-Sybil filter is the reader's choice, not\n   ours). If the score clears its bar, it proceeds.\n4. **Call.** `GET /v1/forecast?lat=..&lon=..` with no credentials → `402` with a\n   `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` header listing accepted payment requirements: `scheme=exact`,\n   `network=eip155:8453`, `token=USDC`, `amount=350000` (6 decimals = $0.35), `payTo`,\n   `maxTimeoutSeconds`, `nonce`.\n5. **Pay.** Client signs an EIP-3009 `TransferWithAuthorization` EIP-712 struct and\n   retries with `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE`. It spends no gas and holds no ETH.\n6. **Verify + settle.** We `POST /verify` to the facilitator (signature, balance,\n   deadline, amount, recipient), then `POST /settle`. The facilitator broadcasts\n   `transferWithAuthorization` on Base and pays the gas.\n7. **Serve.** `200 OK` + forecast, plus `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` carrying the settlement tx\n   hash and a receipt id.\n8. **Rate.** Client calls `ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback(agentId, value, decimals, tag1, tag2, endpoint, ipfsHash, proofHash)`\n   where `proofHash = keccak256(settlement tx hash)`. The receipt body tells it exactly\n   which arguments to use, so this step needs no human interpretation.\n\n### Why verify → settle → serve, in that order\n\nSettlement is ~2s on Base. Serving before settlement (optimistic) would shave latency\nbut lets a caller with a valid-signature/insufficient-balance race extract free data.\nForecast data is cheap but the failure is silent and unbounded, so we settle first.\nIf settlement reverts we return `402` again with a fresh challenge, never `200`.\n\n## 3. Onchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Address / id | Chain | Why we need it | If it fails |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Base L2 | `eip155:8453` | — | Settlement + registry host. Chosen for ~$0.001 gas. | Service unsellable; hard dependency. |\n| USDC | `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` | Base | Payment asset. Must be the EIP-3009 implementation — this is what makes gasless payment possible. | No gasless path; would need permit/approve, which requires caller ETH. |\n| ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry | `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` | Base | Our discoverable identity. `Registered` event is the discovery surface. | New clients can't find us; existing ones still pay. |\n| ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry | `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` | Base | Where callers post ratings and where prospects read them. | Trust signal disappears; payments still work. |\n| Our agentId | ERC-721 tokenId minted at registration | Base | Identity referenced by every rating. **Must be minted before launch.** | — |\n| Treasury address | `PAY_TO` EOA or safe | Base | Receives USDC. Appears in the 402 challenge and is signed over by the payer. | Rotating it invalidates in-flight challenges only. |\n\nDeliberately **not** used: no token of our own, no staking contract, no escrow, no\nsubscription NFT. Each would reintroduce state we'd have to hold for the caller.\n\n## 4. Offchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Role | Failure mode / mitigation |\n|---|---|---|\n| **x402 facilitator** (Coinbase public, or self-hosted) | `POST /verify`, `POST /settle`. Owns gas management and tx broadcast. | Single point of failure for revenue. Mitigation: configure a primary + fallback facilitator URL; self-host if margin justifies running a funded hot wallet. |\n| **IPFS pinning** (Pinata/web3.storage/own node) | Hosts the registration JSON that `agentURI` points at. | If unpinned, `agentURI` dead-ends and clients can't evaluate us. Pin on ≥2 providers; content is immutable so caching is safe. |\n| **Base RPC** (Alchemy/QuickNode/own node) | Reading registry state, confirming settlement, serving receipts. | Read-only; degrade to cached values. |\n| **Upstream weather source** | The actual product. | If it's down we must return `502` **before** settling, so we never charge for a call we can't fulfil. |\n| **DNS + TLS for forecast.example.com** | The `.well-known` domain proof is only as strong as domain control. | Domain hijack = identity hijack. Registrar lock + CAA records. |\n| **Third-party indexer** (The Graph subgraph, 8004 explorers, or client's own log scan) | Makes discovery fast. | We depend on *some* indexer existing, but not a specific one, and we operate none of them. A client can always scan `Registered` logs directly. |\n| **Replay/idempotency store** (in-memory now, Redis in prod) | Rejects reused authorization nonces before we spend a settle call. | USDC enforces nonce uniqueness onchain regardless, so this is a cost optimisation, not a correctness requirement. |\n\n### The gas gap on the rating step — worth being explicit about\n\nPayment is gasless for the caller. **Posting feedback is not** — `giveFeedback` is a\nreal transaction and needs ETH. A caller that holds only USDC can pay us and then be\nunable to rate us. Options, all client-side:\n\n- ERC-4337 account with a paymaster that accepts USDC for gas;\n- EIP-7702 delegated EOA with a sponsored relay;\n- keep a dust ETH balance purely for reputation writes.\n\nWe deliberately do **not** sponsor or relay feedback transactions for our own callers.\nPaying the gas for our own ratings would make the reputation signal worthless to\nexactly the readers we need to convince. We surface the constraint in the agent card\n(`feedback.gasRequired: true`) so a client can plan for it before its first call.\n\n## 5. What we publish, and where\n\n**On IPFS** — the ERC-8004 registration file, referenced by `agentURI`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n  \"name\": \"forecast.example.com\",\n  \"description\": \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35/call, USDC on Base, x402.\",\n  \"services\": [\n    { \"name\": \"A2A\", \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json\", \"version\": \"0.3.0\" }\n  ],\n  \"x402Support\": true,\n  \"active\": true,\n  \"supportedTrust\": [\"reputation\"]\n}\n```\n\n**On the web server** (all unpaywalled — a prospect must be able to evaluate us for free):\n\n| Path | Contents |\n|---|---|\n| `/.well-known/agent-registration.json` | `{ agentId, agentRegistry: \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169…\", owner }` — the domain↔identity proof from step 2. |\n| `/.well-known/agent-card.json` | A2A card: skills, input/output schema, the x402 payment extension (network, token, amount, payTo), and where to post feedback. |\n| `/.well-known/x402` | Machine-readable price list per route. Convenience mirror of the card; the authoritative price is always the live `402` challenge. |\n| `/openapi.json` | Full request/response schema so a client can self-integrate without reading prose. |\n| `/health` | Liveness, for reputation-scoring uptime probes. |\n\n**Onchain** — nothing beyond the registration tx. No price oracle, no listing fee.\n\n## 6. Registration checklist (before the service is findable)\n\n1. Fund an owner wallet on Base with a few dollars of ETH (registration gas only).\n2. Pin `registration.json` to IPFS → `ipfs://Qm…`.\n3. `IdentityRegistry.register(\"ipfs://Qm…\", \"\")` on Base → returns `agentId` (ERC-721 tokenId).\n4. Write that `agentId` + owner into `/.well-known/agent-registration.json` and deploy.\n   **Order matters** — publish the proof before advertising, or early clients fail step 2\n   and may cache us as unverified.\n5. Confirm the `Registered` event is indexed by at least one public indexer.\n6. *(Optional)* ENS text record on `forecast.eth` pointing at the agentId — a second\n   resolution path, not a requirement.\n7. Bootstrap reputation: the registry starts empty and `getSummary` returns count 0.\n   Clients with a strict threshold will skip us. Run real paid calls from independent\n   agents that then rate us honestly. Do **not** self-rate from wallets we control —\n   readers filter by their own trusted-client sets and Sybil ratings are worth nothing.\n\n## 7. Security notes\n\n- **Replay** — every authorization carries a unique `nonce`; USDC marks it used onchain,\n  so a replayed `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` fails at settlement. Local nonce cache just avoids\n  wasting a facilitator call.\n- **Amount/recipient substitution** — we re-check `value >= 350000`, `to == PAY_TO`,\n  `token == USDC`, `chainId == 8453` against the *decoded authorization*, never against\n  client-supplied metadata.\n- **Deadline** — reject `validBefore` within 30s of now; the settle tx needs headroom.\n- **Facilitator trust** — the facilitator can censor or delay but cannot redirect funds:\n  the recipient is inside the caller's signature. Reconcile settled tx hashes against\n  the treasury's USDC transfer logs and alarm on divergence.\n- **Not charging for failures** — upstream is called and validated before settlement;\n  a `5xx` from upstream returns `502` with no charge.\n- **DoS on the free surface** — `.well-known` and `402` challenge generation are\n  unpriced and therefore the cheapest thing to flood. Rate-limit by IP and keep\n  challenge generation allocation-free (no onchain reads on that path).\n\n## 8. Economics\n\n- Revenue: $0.35/call. Settlement gas on Base ≈ $0.001–0.005, paid by the facilitator\n  and priced into its fee.\n- Registration: one tx, cents.\n- Upstream weather data is the real COGS — must stay well under $0.35 per call for the\n  unit to work. Cache aggressively by (lat, lon, hour); the paywall is per call, but\n  nothing requires a distinct upstream fetch per call.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdSTR8fqVJR35z7MVt3xT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TwbV6vBBL1nVLFNCMrMZer","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T110521Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/server.ts","content":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — x402 + ERC-8004 weather service.\n *\n * Sells point forecasts to autonomous agents for $0.35 in USDC on Base.\n * No accounts, no API keys, no human in the loop. See design.md.\n *\n * The x402 flow is implemented explicitly against the facilitator's\n * /verify + /settle endpoints rather than dropped in via @x402/express,\n * because we attach a settlement receipt and ERC-8004 feedback instructions\n * to every paid response.\n */\n\nimport express, { NextFunction, Request, Response } from \"express\";\nimport { getAddress, isAddress, keccak256, toHex } from \"viem\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Config\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Base mainnet. */\nconst CHAIN_ID = 8453;\nconst NETWORK = `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}`;\n\n/** USDC on Base — the EIP-3009 implementation. */\nconst USDC = getAddress(\"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\");\n\n/** ERC-8004 registries. Same addresses on every supported chain. */\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = getAddress(\"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\");\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = getAddress(\"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\");\n\n/** $0.35, USDC has 6 decimals. */\nconst PRICE_ATOMIC = \"350000\";\nconst PRICE_USD = \"0.35\";\n\nconst ORIGIN = process.env.ORIGIN ?? \"https://forecast.example.com\";\nconst PORT = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3000);\n\n/** Treasury that receives USDC. Signed over by the payer — never mutable per-request. */\nconst PAY_TO = getAddress(\n  process.env.PAY_TO ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\",\n);\n\n/** Our ERC-8004 agent identity (ERC-721 tokenId), minted before launch. */\nconst AGENT_ID = process.env.AGENT_ID ?? \"0\";\n/** Wallet that owns the agentId NFT. Clients cross-check this against ownerOf(). */\nconst AGENT_OWNER = getAddress(\n  process.env.AGENT_OWNER ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\",\n);\n/** IPFS URI of the registration JSON that agentURI points at. */\nconst AGENT_URI = process.env.AGENT_URI ?? \"ipfs://QmReplaceWithRegistrationCid\";\n\n/** x402 facilitator. Handles gas so neither we nor the caller need ETH. */\nconst FACILITATOR = process.env.FACILITATOR_URL ?? \"https://x402.org/facilitator\";\n\n/** Reject authorizations expiring within this window — settlement needs headroom. */\nconst MIN_DEADLINE_SLACK_SEC = 30;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface PaymentRequirements {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  token: string;\n  amount: string;\n  payTo: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  nonce: string;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n}\n\n/** Decoded PAYMENT-SIGNATURE payload — an EIP-3009 authorization plus routing info. */\ninterface PaymentPayload {\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  token: string;\n  amount: string;\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  signature: string;\n  nonce: string;\n  /** EIP-3009 validBefore, unix seconds. `deadline` accepted as an alias. */\n  validBefore?: number;\n  deadline?: number;\n  validAfter?: number;\n}\n\ninterface SettlementResult {\n  success: boolean;\n  txHash?: string;\n  networkId?: string;\n  errorReason?: string;\n}\n\ninterface Receipt {\n  receiptId: string;\n  txHash: string;\n  payer: string;\n  amount: string;\n  token: string;\n  network: string;\n  resource: string;\n  settledAt: string;\n  /** Everything the caller needs to post an ERC-8004 rating without asking a human. */\n  feedback: FeedbackInstructions;\n}\n\ninterface FeedbackInstructions {\n  registry: string;\n  chainId: number;\n  agentId: string;\n  method: string;\n  endpoint: string;\n  /** keccak256 of the settlement tx hash — binds the rating to a real payment. */\n  proofHash: string;\n  suggestedTags: [string, string];\n  gasRequired: true;\n  note: string;\n}\n\ndeclare global {\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-namespace\n  namespace Express {\n    interface Request {\n      receipt?: Receipt;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Small stores (swap for Redis in production — see design.md §4)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst NONCE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;\nconst seenNonces = new Map<string, number>();\nconst receipts = new Map<string, Receipt>();\n\nfunction markNonceUsed(nonce: string): boolean {\n  const now = Date.now();\n  for (const [k, t] of seenNonces) if (now - t > NONCE_TTL_MS) seenNonces.delete(k);\n  const key = nonce.toLowerCase();\n  if (seenNonces.has(key)) return false;\n  seenNonces.set(key, now);\n  return true;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction b64encode(value: unknown): string {\n  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value), \"utf8\").toString(\"base64\");\n}\n\nfunction b64decode<T>(value: string): T {\n  return JSON.parse(Buffer.from(value, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\")) as T;\n}\n\nfunction randomNonce(): string {\n  return toHex(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)));\n}\n\nfunction buildRequirements(req: Request): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: NETWORK,\n    token: USDC,\n    amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n    payTo: PAY_TO,\n    resource: `${ORIGIN}${req.originalUrl}`,\n    description: `Point weather forecast — $${PRICE_USD} per call`,\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    nonce: randomNonce(),\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: 120,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction sendPaymentRequired(req: Request, res: Response, reason?: string): void {\n  const requirements = buildRequirements(req);\n  const body = {\n    x402Version: 1,\n    error: reason ?? \"payment required\",\n    accepts: [requirements],\n  };\n  res\n    .status(402)\n    .set(\"PAYMENT-REQUIRED\", b64encode(body))\n    .json(body);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Facilitator client — owns gas and tx broadcast so the caller needs no ETH\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function facilitatorCall<T>(\n  path: string,\n  payload: PaymentPayload,\n  requirements: PaymentRequirements,\n): Promise<T> {\n  const res = await fetch(`${FACILITATOR}${path}`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ x402Version: 1, paymentPayload: payload, paymentRequirements: requirements }),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    throw new Error(`facilitator ${path} failed: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n  }\n  return (await res.json()) as T;\n}\n\nconst verifyPayment = (p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitatorCall<{ isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: string }>(\"/verify\", p, r);\n\nconst settlePayment = (p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitatorCall<SettlementResult>(\"/settle\", p, r);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Local validation — never trust the facilitator alone on amount or recipient\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction validateLocally(payload: PaymentPayload): string | null {\n  if (payload.scheme !== \"exact\") return `unsupported scheme ${payload.scheme}`;\n  if (payload.network !== NETWORK) return `wrong network, expected ${NETWORK}`;\n\n  if (!isAddress(payload.token) || getAddress(payload.token) !== USDC) {\n    return \"wrong token, expected USDC on Base\";\n  }\n  if (!isAddress(payload.to) || getAddress(payload.to) !== PAY_TO) {\n    return \"wrong recipient\";\n  }\n  if (!isAddress(payload.from)) return \"invalid payer address\";\n\n  let amount: bigint;\n  try {\n    amount = BigInt(payload.amount);\n  } catch {\n    return \"amount is not an integer\";\n  }\n  if (amount < BigInt(PRICE_ATOMIC)) return `underpaid, need ${PRICE_ATOMIC} atomic USDC`;\n\n  const validBefore = payload.validBefore ?? payload.deadline;\n  if (!validBefore) return \"missing validBefore\";\n  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\n  if (validBefore < now + MIN_DEADLINE_SLACK_SEC) return \"authorization expires too soon\";\n  if (payload.validAfter && payload.validAfter > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n\n  if (!payload.nonce || !payload.signature) return \"missing nonce or signature\";\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// x402 middleware\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction requirePayment(handler: (req: Request) => Promise<unknown>) {\n  return async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {\n    const header = req.header(\"PAYMENT-SIGNATURE\");\n    if (!header) return sendPaymentRequired(req, res);\n\n    let payload: PaymentPayload;\n    try {\n      payload = b64decode<PaymentPayload>(header);\n    } catch {\n      return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, \"PAYMENT-SIGNATURE is not valid base64 JSON\");\n    }\n\n    const localError = validateLocally(payload);\n    if (localError) return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, localError);\n\n    // Cheap replay screen. USDC enforces nonce uniqueness onchain regardless;\n    // this just avoids burning a facilitator round-trip.\n    if (!markNonceUsed(payload.nonce)) {\n      return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, \"authorization nonce already used\");\n    }\n\n    const requirements = buildRequirements(req);\n\n    try {\n      const verification = await verifyPayment(payload, requirements);\n      if (!verification.isValid) {\n        return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment invalid\");\n      }\n\n      // Produce the product BEFORE settling: if upstream is down we must not charge.\n      const result = await handler(req);\n\n      const settlement = await settlePayment(payload, requirements);\n      if (!settlement.success || !settlement.txHash) {\n        return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, settlement.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\");\n      }\n\n      const receipt = buildReceipt(payload, settlement.txHash, requirements);\n      receipts.set(receipt.receiptId, receipt);\n\n      res\n        .status(200)\n        .set(\"PAYMENT-RESPONSE\", b64encode({ success: true, transaction: settlement.txHash, network: NETWORK, payer: payload.from }))\n        .json({ data: result, receipt });\n    } catch (err) {\n      next(err);\n    }\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildReceipt(\n  payload: PaymentPayload,\n  txHash: string,\n  requirements: PaymentRequirements,\n): Receipt {\n  return {\n    receiptId: payload.nonce,\n    txHash,\n    payer: getAddress(payload.from),\n    amount: payload.amount,\n    token: USDC,\n    network: NETWORK,\n    resource: requirements.resource,\n    settledAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    feedback: {\n      registry: `${NETWORK}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      chainId: CHAIN_ID,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      method:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId, int128 value, uint8 valueDecimals, string tag1, string tag2, string endpoint, string ipfsHash, bytes32 proofHash)\",\n      endpoint: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n      proofHash: keccak256(txHash as `0x${string}`),\n      suggestedTags: [\"quality\", \"weather\"],\n      gasRequired: true,\n      note:\n        \"Rate 0-100 with valueDecimals=0. giveFeedback is a real transaction and costs gas; \" +\n        \"use an ERC-4337 USDC paymaster or an EIP-7702 sponsored relay if you hold no ETH. \" +\n        \"We do not sponsor feedback transactions for our own callers.\",\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The product\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface Forecast {\n  location: { lat: number; lon: number };\n  issuedAt: string;\n  units: { temperature: \"celsius\"; wind: \"m/s\"; precipitation: \"mm\" };\n  hourly: Array<{ time: string; temperature: number; wind: number; precipitation: number; condition: string }>;\n}\n\nclass UpstreamError extends Error {}\n\n/**\n * Fetch from the upstream weather source. Cache by (lat, lon, hour) — the paywall\n * is per call, but nothing requires a distinct upstream fetch per call.\n */\nasync function getForecast(lat: number, lon: number, hours: number): Promise<Forecast> {\n  const base = process.env.UPSTREAM_WEATHER_URL;\n  if (!base) throw new UpstreamError(\"UPSTREAM_WEATHER_URL is not configured\");\n\n  const url = new URL(base);\n  url.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(lat));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(lon));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"hourly\", \"temperature_2m,wind_speed_10m,precipitation,weather_code\");\n  url.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(hours));\n\n  const res = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new UpstreamError(`upstream returned ${res.status}`);\n\n  const raw = (await res.json()) as {\n    hourly: {\n      time: string[];\n      temperature_2m: number[];\n      wind_speed_10m: number[];\n      precipitation: number[];\n      weather_code: number[];\n    };\n  };\n\n  if (!raw?.hourly?.time?.length) throw new UpstreamError(\"upstream returned no hourly data\");\n\n  return {\n    location: { lat, lon },\n    issuedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    units: { temperature: \"celsius\", wind: \"m/s\", precipitation: \"mm\" },\n    hourly: raw.hourly.time.slice(0, hours).map((time, i) => ({\n      time,\n      temperature: raw.hourly.temperature_2m[i],\n      wind: raw.hourly.wind_speed_10m[i],\n      precipitation: raw.hourly.precipitation[i],\n      condition: describeCode(raw.hourly.weather_code[i]),\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction describeCode(code: number): string {\n  if (code === 0) return \"clear\";\n  if (code <= 3) return \"partly-cloudy\";\n  if (code <= 48) return \"fog\";\n  if (code <= 67) return \"rain\";\n  if (code <= 77) return \"snow\";\n  if (code <= 82) return \"showers\";\n  if (code <= 86) return \"snow-showers\";\n  return \"thunderstorm\";\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// App\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst app = express();\napp.disable(\"x-powered-by\");\napp.use(express.json({ limit: \"16kb\" }));\n\n// --- Free, unpaywalled discovery surface ----------------------------------\n// A prospect must be able to evaluate us end to end without paying.\n\n/** Proves the holder of the agentId controls this domain. Checked before trusting us. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-registration.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    agentId: AGENT_ID,\n    agentRegistry: `${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`,\n    owner: AGENT_OWNER,\n    agentURI: AGENT_URI,\n  });\n});\n\n/** A2A agent card — machine-readable capability + pricing description. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-card.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. Per-call payment, no accounts.\",\n    url: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    preferredTransport: \"JSONRPC\",\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, stateTransitionHistory: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly temperature, wind and precipitation for a lat/lon, up to 168 hours ahead.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"meteorology\"],\n        examples: [`${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.41&hours=24`],\n      },\n    ],\n    registrations: [\n      { agentId: AGENT_ID, agentRegistry: `${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`, signature: null },\n    ],\n    extensions: [\n      {\n        uri: \"https://www.x402.org/extension/v1\",\n        description: `$${PRICE_USD} USDC per call on Base, settled via EIP-3009. Caller needs no ETH.`,\n        params: {\n          scheme: \"exact\",\n          network: NETWORK,\n          token: USDC,\n          amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n          decimals: 6,\n          payTo: PAY_TO,\n        },\n      },\n    ],\n    feedback: {\n      registry: `${NETWORK}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      tags: [\"quality\", \"weather\"],\n      gasRequired: true,\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n/** Convenience price list. The live 402 challenge is always authoritative. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/x402\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    x402Version: 1,\n    resources: [\n      {\n        resource: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n        method: \"GET\",\n        accepts: [\n          { scheme: \"exact\", network: NETWORK, token: USDC, amount: PRICE_ATOMIC, payTo: PAY_TO },\n        ],\n        description: `Point weather forecast — $${PRICE_USD} per call`,\n      },\n    ],\n  });\n});\n\napp.get(\"/openapi.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    openapi: \"3.1.0\",\n    info: {\n      title: \"forecast.example.com\",\n      version: \"1.0.0\",\n      description: `Weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $${PRICE_USD} USDC per call on Base via x402. No API keys.`,\n    },\n    servers: [{ url: ORIGIN }],\n    paths: {\n      \"/v1/forecast\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Point forecast\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"lat\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\", minimum: -90, maximum: 90 } },\n            { name: \"lon\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\", minimum: -180, maximum: 180 } },\n            { name: \"hours\", in: \"query\", required: false, schema: { type: \"integer\", minimum: 1, maximum: 168, default: 24 } },\n          ],\n          responses: {\n            \"200\": { description: \"Forecast plus settlement receipt\" },\n            \"400\": { description: \"Invalid coordinates\" },\n            \"402\": {\n              description: \"Payment required — see the PAYMENT-REQUIRED header for accepted terms\",\n            },\n            \"502\": { description: \"Upstream weather source unavailable; no charge was made\" },\n          },\n        },\n      },\n      \"/v1/receipts/{receiptId}\": {\n        get: { summary: \"Re-fetch a settlement receipt\", responses: { \"200\": { description: \"Receipt\" } } },\n      },\n    },\n  });\n});\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({ status: \"ok\", agentId: AGENT_ID, network: NETWORK, price: PRICE_ATOMIC });\n});\n\n// --- Paid route ------------------------------------------------------------\n\napp.get(\n  \"/v1/forecast\",\n  requirePayment(async (req) => {\n    const lat = Number(req.query.lat);\n    const lon = Number(req.query.lon);\n    const hours = req.query.hours === undefined ? 24 : Number(req.query.hours);\n\n    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) throw new BadRequest(\"lat must be between -90 and 90\");\n    if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) throw new BadRequest(\"lon must be between -180 and 180\");\n    if (!Number.isInteger(hours) || hours < 1 || hours > 168) throw new BadRequest(\"hours must be an integer 1-168\");\n\n    return getForecast(lat, lon, hours);\n  }),\n);\n\n/** Receipts are re-fetchable so a caller can post feedback after a crash or restart. */\napp.get(\"/v1/receipts/:receiptId\", (req, res) => {\n  const receipt = receipts.get(req.params.receiptId);\n  if (!receipt) return res.status(404).json({ error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n  res.json(receipt);\n});\n\n// --- Errors ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass BadRequest extends Error {}\n\napp.use((err: Error, _req: Request, res: Response, _next: NextFunction) => {\n  if (err instanceof BadRequest) return res.status(400).json({ error: err.message });\n  // Upstream failure surfaces before settlement, so nothing was charged.\n  if (err instanceof UpstreamError) {\n    return res.status(502).json({ error: \"upstream weather source unavailable\", charged: false });\n  }\n  console.error(err);\n  res.status(500).json({ error: \"internal error\", charged: false });\n});\n\nif (PAY_TO === \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\") {\n  console.warn(\"PAY_TO is unset — payments would be signed to the zero address. Set it before serving traffic.\");\n}\n\napp.listen(PORT, () => {\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${PORT}`);\n  console.log(`  agentId ${AGENT_ID} @ ${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`);\n  console.log(`  $${PRICE_USD} USDC/call → ${PAY_TO} via ${FACILITATOR}`);\n});\n\nexport default app;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8934,"cache_read_input_tokens":29347,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":8934},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb3c5f5c-06db-42d8-b4da-0e3a5dc961d2","uuid":"0a1cdb6f-70a7-4dbf-8130-cce1bc8d2dec","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:09:04.867Z","request_id":"req_011CdSTR7RgCWGxa7zUM77HB"}
