See design.md.\n *\n * The x402 flow is implemented explicitly against the facilitator's\n * /verify + /settle endpoints rather than dropped in via @x402/express,\n * because we attach a settlement receipt and ERC-8004 feedback instructions\n * to every paid response.\n */\n\nimport express, { NextFunction, Request, Response } from \"express\";\nimport { getAddress, isAddress, keccak256, toHex } from \"viem\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Config\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Base mainnet. */\nconst CHAIN_ID = 8453;\nconst NETWORK = `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}`;\n\n/** USDC on Base — the EIP-3009 implementation. */\nconst USDC = getAddress(\"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\");\n\n/** ERC-8004 registries. Same addresses on every supported chain. */\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = getAddress(\"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\");\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = getAddress(\"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\");\n\n/** $0.35, USDC has 6 decimals. */\nconst PRICE_ATOMIC = \"350000\";\nconst PRICE_USD = \"0.35\";\n\nconst ORIGIN = process.env.ORIGIN ?? \"https://forecast.example.com\";\nconst PORT = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3000);\n\n/** Treasury that receives USDC. Signed over by the payer — never mutable per-request. */\nconst PAY_TO = getAddress(\n  process.env.PAY_TO ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\",\n);\n\n/** Our ERC-8004 agent identity (ERC-721 tokenId), minted before launch. */\nconst AGENT_ID = process.env.AGENT_ID ?? \"0\";\n/** Wallet that owns the agentId NFT. Clients cross-check this against ownerOf(). */\nconst AGENT_OWNER = getAddress(\n  process.env.AGENT_OWNER ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\",\n);\n/** IPFS URI of the registration JSON that agentURI points at. */\nconst AGENT_URI = process.env.AGENT_URI ?? \"ipfs://QmReplaceWithRegistrationCid\";\n\n/** x402 facilitator. Handles gas so neither we nor the caller need ETH. */\nconst FACILITATOR = process.env.FACILITATOR_URL ?? \"https://x402.org/facilitator\";\n\n/** Reject authorizations expiring within this window — settlement needs headroom. */\nconst MIN_DEADLINE_SLACK_SEC = 30;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface PaymentRequirements {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  token: string;\n  amount: string;\n  payTo: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  nonce: string;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n}\n\n/** Decoded PAYMENT-SIGNATURE payload — an EIP-3009 authorization plus routing info. */\ninterface PaymentPayload {\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  token: string;\n  amount: string;\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  signature: string;\n  nonce: string;\n  /** EIP-3009 validBefore, unix seconds. `deadline` accepted as an alias. */\n  validBefore?: number;\n  deadline?: number;\n  validAfter?: number;\n}\n\ninterface SettlementResult {\n  success: boolean;\n  txHash?: string;\n  networkId?: string;\n  errorReason?: string;\n}\n\ninterface Receipt {\n  receiptId: string;\n  txHash: string;\n  payer: string;\n  amount: string;\n  token: string;\n  network: string;\n  resource: string;\n  settledAt: string;\n  /** Everything the caller needs to post an ERC-8004 rating without asking a human. */\n  feedback: FeedbackInstructions;\n}\n\ninterface FeedbackInstructions {\n  registry: string;\n  chainId: number;\n  agentId: string;\n  method: string;\n  endpoint: string;\n  /** keccak256 of the settlement tx hash — binds the rating to a real payment. */\n  proofHash: string;\n  suggestedTags: [string, string];\n  gasRequired: true;\n  note: string;\n}\n\ndeclare global {\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-namespace\n  namespace Express {\n    interface Request {\n      receipt?: Receipt;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Small stores (swap for Redis in production — see design.md §4)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst NONCE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;\nconst seenNonces = new Map<string, number>();\nconst receipts = new Map<string, Receipt>();\n\nfunction markNonceUsed(nonce: string): boolean {\n  const now = Date.now();\n  for (const [k, t] of seenNonces) if (now - t > NONCE_TTL_MS) seenNonces.delete(k);\n  const key = nonce.toLowerCase();\n  if (seenNonces.has(key)) return false;\n  seenNonces.set(key, now);\n  return true;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction b64encode(value: unknown): string {\n  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value), \"utf8\").toString(\"base64\");\n}\n\nfunction b64decode<T>(value: string): T {\n  return JSON.parse(Buffer.from(value, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\")) as T;\n}\n\nfunction randomNonce(): string {\n  return toHex(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)));\n}\n\nfunction buildRequirements(req: Request): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: NETWORK,\n    token: USDC,\n    amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n    payTo: PAY_TO,\n    resource: `${ORIGIN}${req.originalUrl}`,\n    description: `Point weather forecast — $${PRICE_USD} per call`,\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    nonce: randomNonce(),\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: 120,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction sendPaymentRequired(req: Request, res: Response, reason?: string): void {\n  const requirements = buildRequirements(req);\n  const body = {\n    x402Version: 1,\n    error: reason ?? \"payment required\",\n    accepts: [requirements],\n  };\n  res\n    .status(402)\n    .set(\"PAYMENT-REQUIRED\", b64encode(body))\n    .json(body);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Facilitator client — owns gas and tx broadcast so the caller needs no ETH\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function facilitatorCall<T>(\n  path: string,\n  payload: PaymentPayload,\n  requirements: PaymentRequirements,\n): Promise<T> {\n  const res = await fetch(`${FACILITATOR}${path}`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ x402Version: 1, paymentPayload: payload, paymentRequirements: requirements }),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    throw new Error(`facilitator ${path} failed: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n  }\n  return (await res.json()) as T;\n}\n\nconst verifyPayment = (p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitatorCall<{ isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: string }>(\"/verify\", p, r);\n\nconst settlePayment = (p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitatorCall<SettlementResult>(\"/settle\", p, r);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Local validation — never trust the facilitator alone on amount or recipient\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction validateLocally(payload: PaymentPayload): string | null {\n  if (payload.scheme !== \"exact\") return `unsupported scheme ${payload.scheme}`;\n  if (payload.network !== NETWORK) return `wrong network, expected ${NETWORK}`;\n\n  if (!isAddress(payload.token) || getAddress(payload.token) !== USDC) {\n    return \"wrong token, expected USDC on Base\";\n  }\n  if (!isAddress(payload.to) || getAddress(payload.to) !== PAY_TO) {\n    return \"wrong recipient\";\n  }\n  if (!isAddress(payload.from)) return \"invalid payer address\";\n\n  let amount: bigint;\n  try {\n    amount = BigInt(payload.amount);\n  } catch {\n    return \"amount is not an integer\";\n  }\n  if (amount < BigInt(PRICE_ATOMIC)) return `underpaid, need ${PRICE_ATOMIC} atomic USDC`;\n\n  const validBefore = payload.validBefore ?? payload.deadline;\n  if (!validBefore) return \"missing validBefore\";\n  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\n  if (validBefore < now + MIN_DEADLINE_SLACK_SEC) return \"authorization expires too soon\";\n  if (payload.validAfter && payload.validAfter > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n\n  if (!payload.nonce || !payload.signature) return \"missing nonce or signature\";\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// x402 middleware\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction requirePayment(handler: (req: Request) => Promise<unknown>) {\n  return async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {\n    const header = req.header(\"PAYMENT-SIGNATURE\");\n    if (!header) return sendPaymentRequired(req, res);\n\n    let payload: PaymentPayload;\n    try {\n      payload = b64decode<PaymentPayload>(header);\n    } catch {\n      return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, \"PAYMENT-SIGNATURE is not valid base64 JSON\");\n    }\n\n    const localError = validateLocally(payload);\n    if (localError) return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, localError);\n\n    // Cheap replay screen. USDC enforces nonce uniqueness onchain regardless;\n    // this just avoids burning a facilitator round-trip.\n    if (!markNonceUsed(payload.nonce)) {\n      return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, \"authorization nonce already used\");\n    }\n\n    const requirements = buildRequirements(req);\n\n    try {\n      const verification = await verifyPayment(payload, requirements);\n      if (!verification.isValid) {\n        return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment invalid\");\n      }\n\n      // Produce the product BEFORE settling: if upstream is down we must not charge.\n      const result = await handler(req);\n\n      const settlement = await settlePayment(payload, requirements);\n      if (!settlement.success || !settlement.txHash) {\n        return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, settlement.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\");\n      }\n\n      const receipt = buildReceipt(payload, settlement.txHash, requirements);\n      receipts.set(receipt.receiptId, receipt);\n\n      res\n        .status(200)\n        .set(\"PAYMENT-RESPONSE\", b64encode({ success: true, transaction: settlement.txHash, network: NETWORK, payer: payload.from }))\n        .json({ data: result, receipt });\n    } catch (err) {\n      next(err);\n    }\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildReceipt(\n  payload: PaymentPayload,\n  txHash: string,\n  requirements: PaymentRequirements,\n): Receipt {\n  return {\n    receiptId: payload.nonce,\n    txHash,\n    payer: getAddress(payload.from),\n    amount: payload.amount,\n    token: USDC,\n    network: NETWORK,\n    resource: requirements.resource,\n    settledAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    feedback: {\n      registry: `${NETWORK}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      chainId: CHAIN_ID,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      method:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId, int128 value, uint8 valueDecimals, string tag1, string tag2, string endpoint, string ipfsHash, bytes32 proofHash)\",\n      endpoint: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n      proofHash: keccak256(txHash as `0x${string}`),\n      suggestedTags: [\"quality\", \"weather\"],\n      gasRequired: true,\n      note:\n        \"Rate 0-100 with valueDecimals=0. giveFeedback is a real transaction and costs gas; \" +\n        \"use an ERC-4337 USDC paymaster or an EIP-7702 sponsored relay if you hold no ETH. \" +\n        \"We do not sponsor feedback transactions for our own callers.\",\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The product\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface Forecast {\n  location: { lat: number; lon: number };\n  issuedAt: string;\n  units: { temperature: \"celsius\"; wind: \"m/s\"; precipitation: \"mm\" };\n  hourly: Array<{ time: string; temperature: number; wind: number; precipitation: number; condition: string }>;\n}\n\nclass UpstreamError extends Error {}\n\n/**\n * Fetch from the upstream weather source. Cache by (lat, lon, hour) — the paywall\n * is per call, but nothing requires a distinct upstream fetch per call.\n */\nasync function getForecast(lat: number, lon: number, hours: number): Promise<Forecast> {\n  const base = process.env.UPSTREAM_WEATHER_URL;\n  if (!base) throw new UpstreamError(\"UPSTREAM_WEATHER_URL is not configured\");\n\n  const url = new URL(base);\n  url.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(lat));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(lon));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"hourly\", \"temperature_2m,wind_speed_10m,precipitation,weather_code\");\n  url.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(hours));\n\n  const res = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new UpstreamError(`upstream returned ${res.status}`);\n\n  const raw = (await res.json()) as {\n    hourly: {\n      time: string[];\n      temperature_2m: number[];\n      wind_speed_10m: number[];\n      precipitation: number[];\n      weather_code: number[];\n    };\n  };\n\n  if (!raw?.hourly?.time?.length) throw new UpstreamError(\"upstream returned no hourly data\");\n\n  return {\n    location: { lat, lon },\n    issuedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    units: { temperature: \"celsius\", wind: \"m/s\", precipitation: \"mm\" },\n    hourly: raw.hourly.time.slice(0, hours).map((time, i) => ({\n      time,\n      temperature: raw.hourly.temperature_2m[i],\n      wind: raw.hourly.wind_speed_10m[i],\n      precipitation: raw.hourly.precipitation[i],\n      condition: describeCode(raw.hourly.weather_code[i]),\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction describeCode(code: number): string {\n  if (code === 0) return \"clear\";\n  if (code <= 3) return \"partly-cloudy\";\n  if (code <= 48) return \"fog\";\n  if (code <= 67) return \"rain\";\n  if (code <= 77) return \"snow\";\n  if (code <= 82) return \"showers\";\n  if (code <= 86) return \"snow-showers\";\n  return \"thunderstorm\";\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// App\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst app = express();\napp.disable(\"x-powered-by\");\napp.use(express.json({ limit: \"16kb\" }));\n\n// --- Free, unpaywalled discovery surface ----------------------------------\n// A prospect must be able to evaluate us end to end without paying.\n\n/** Proves the holder of the agentId controls this domain. Checked before trusting us. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-registration.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    agentId: AGENT_ID,\n    agentRegistry: `${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`,\n    owner: AGENT_OWNER,\n    agentURI: AGENT_URI,\n  });\n});\n\n/** A2A agent card — machine-readable capability + pricing description. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-card.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. Per-call payment, no accounts.\",\n    url: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    preferredTransport: \"JSONRPC\",\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, stateTransitionHistory: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly temperature, wind and precipitation for a lat/lon, up to 168 hours ahead.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"meteorology\"],\n        examples: [`${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.41&hours=24`],\n      },\n    ],\n    registrations: [\n      { agentId: AGENT_ID, agentRegistry: `${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`, signature: null },\n    ],\n    extensions: [\n      {\n        uri: \"https://www.x402.org/extension/v1\",\n        description: `$${PRICE_USD} USDC per call on Base, settled via EIP-3009. Caller needs no ETH.`,\n        params: {\n          scheme: \"exact\",\n          network: NETWORK,\n          token: USDC,\n          amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n          decimals: 6,\n          payTo: PAY_TO,\n        },\n      },\n    ],\n    feedback: {\n      registry: `${NETWORK}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      tags: [\"quality\", \"weather\"],\n      gasRequired: true,\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n/** Convenience price list. The live 402 challenge is always authoritative. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/x402\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    x402Version: 1,\n    resources: [\n      {\n        resource: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n        method: \"GET\",\n        accepts: [\n          { scheme: \"exact\", network: NETWORK, token: USDC, amount: PRICE_ATOMIC, payTo: PAY_TO },\n        ],\n        description: `Point weather forecast — $${PRICE_USD} per call`,\n      },\n    ],\n  });\n});\n\napp.get(\"/openapi.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    openapi: \"3.1.0\",\n    info: {\n      title: \"forecast.example.com\",\n      version: \"1.0.0\",\n      description: `Weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $${PRICE_USD} USDC per call on Base via x402. No API keys.`,\n    },\n    servers: [{ url: ORIGIN }],\n    paths: {\n      \"/v1/forecast\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Point forecast\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"lat\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\", minimum: -90, maximum: 90 } },\n            { name: \"lon\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\", minimum: -180, maximum: 180 } },\n            { name: \"hours\", in: \"query\", required: false, schema: { type: \"integer\", minimum: 1, maximum: 168, default: 24 } },\n          ],\n          responses: {\n            \"200\": { description: \"Forecast plus settlement receipt\" },\n            \"400\": { description: \"Invalid coordinates\" },\n            \"402\": {\n              description: \"Payment required — see the PAYMENT-REQUIRED header for accepted terms\",\n            },\n            \"502\": { description: \"Upstream weather source unavailable; no charge was made\" },\n          },\n        },\n      },\n      \"/v1/receipts/{receiptId}\": {\n        get: { summary: \"Re-fetch a settlement receipt\", responses: { \"200\": { description: \"Receipt\" } } },\n      },\n    },\n  });\n});\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({ status: \"ok\", agentId: AGENT_ID, network: NETWORK, price: PRICE_ATOMIC });\n});\n\n// --- Paid route ------------------------------------------------------------\n\napp.get(\n  \"/v1/forecast\",\n  requirePayment(async (req) => {\n    const lat = Number(req.query.lat);\n    const lon = Number(req.query.lon);\n    const hours = req.query.hours === undefined ? 24 : Number(req.query.hours);\n\n    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) throw new BadRequest(\"lat must be between -90 and 90\");\n    if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) throw new BadRequest(\"lon must be between -180 and 180\");\n    if (!Number.isInteger(hours) || hours < 1 || hours > 168) throw new BadRequest(\"hours must be an integer 1-168\");\n\n    return getForecast(lat, lon, hours);\n  }),\n);\n\n/** Receipts are re-fetchable so a caller can post feedback after a crash or restart. */\napp.get(\"/v1/receipts/:receiptId\", (req, res) => {\n  const receipt = receipts.get(req.params.receiptId);\n  if (!receipt) return res.status(404).json({ error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n  res.json(receipt);\n});\n\n// --- Errors ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass BadRequest extends Error {}\n\napp.use((err: Error, _req: Request, res: Response, _next: NextFunction) => {\n  if (err instanceof BadRequest) return res.status(400).json({ error: err.message });\n  // Upstream failure surfaces before settlement, so nothing was charged.\n  if (err instanceof UpstreamError) {\n    return res.status(502).json({ error: \"upstream weather source unavailable\", charged: false });\n  }\n  console.error(err);\n  res.status(500).json({ error: \"internal error\", charged: false });\n});\n\nif (PAY_TO === \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\") {\n  console.warn(\"PAY_TO is unset — payments would be signed to the zero address. Set it before serving traffic.\");\n}\n\napp.listen(PORT, () => {\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${PORT}`);\n  console.log(`  agentId ${AGENT_ID} @ ${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`);\n  console.log(`  $${PRICE_USD} USDC/call → ${PAY_TO} via ${FACILITATOR}`);\n});\n\nexport default app;\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb3c5f5c-06db-42d8-b4da-0e3a5dc961d2","uuid":"507b6a52-df1f-4ea9-b2ab-2d90c5cd3961","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:09:11.171Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T110521Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/server.ts","oldString":"class UpstreamError extends Error {}","newString":"class UpstreamError extends Error {}\nclass BadRequest extends Error {}","originalFile":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — x402 + ERC-8004 weather service.\n *\n * Sells point forecasts to autonomous agents for $0.35 in USDC on Base.\n * No accounts, no API keys, no human in the loop. See design.md.\n *\n * The x402 flow is implemented explicitly against the facilitator's\n * /verify + /settle endpoints rather than dropped in via @x402/express,\n * because we attach a settlement receipt and ERC-8004 feedback instructions\n * to every paid response.\n */\n\nimport express, { NextFunction, Request, Response } from \"express\";\nimport { getAddress, isAddress, keccak256, toHex } from \"viem\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Config\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Base mainnet. */\nconst CHAIN_ID = 8453;\nconst NETWORK = `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}`;\n\n/** USDC on Base — the EIP-3009 implementation. */\nconst USDC = getAddress(\"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\");\n\n/** ERC-8004 registries. Same addresses on every supported chain. */\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = getAddress(\"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\");\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = getAddress(\"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\");\n\n/** $0.35, USDC has 6 decimals. */\nconst PRICE_ATOMIC = \"350000\";\nconst PRICE_USD = \"0.35\";\n\nconst ORIGIN = process.env.ORIGIN ?? \"https://forecast.example.com\";\nconst PORT = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3000);\n\n/** Treasury that receives USDC. Signed over by the payer — never mutable per-request. */\nconst PAY_TO = getAddress(\n  process.env.PAY_TO ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\",\n);\n\n/** Our ERC-8004 agent identity (ERC-721 tokenId), minted before launch. */\nconst AGENT_ID = process.env.AGENT_ID ?? \"0\";\n/** Wallet that owns the agentId NFT. Clients cross-check this against ownerOf(). */\nconst AGENT_OWNER = getAddress(\n  process.env.AGENT_OWNER ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\",\n);\n/** IPFS URI of the registration JSON that agentURI points at. */\nconst AGENT_URI = process.env.AGENT_URI ?? \"ipfs://QmReplaceWithRegistrationCid\";\n\n/** x402 facilitator. Handles gas so neither we nor the caller need ETH. */\nconst FACILITATOR = process.env.FACILITATOR_URL ?? \"https://x402.org/facilitator\";\n\n/** Reject authorizations expiring within this window — settlement needs headroom. */\nconst MIN_DEADLINE_SLACK_SEC = 30;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface PaymentRequirements {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  token: string;\n  amount: string;\n  payTo: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  nonce: string;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n}\n\n/** Decoded PAYMENT-SIGNATURE payload — an EIP-3009 authorization plus routing info. */\ninterface PaymentPayload {\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  token: string;\n  amount: string;\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  signature: string;\n  nonce: string;\n  /** EIP-3009 validBefore, unix seconds. `deadline` accepted as an alias. */\n  validBefore?: number;\n  deadline?: number;\n  validAfter?: number;\n}\n\ninterface SettlementResult {\n  success: boolean;\n  txHash?: string;\n  networkId?: string;\n  errorReason?: string;\n}\n\ninterface Receipt {\n  receiptId: string;\n  txHash: string;\n  payer: string;\n  amount: string;\n  token: string;\n  network: string;\n  resource: string;\n  settledAt: string;\n  /** Everything the caller needs to post an ERC-8004 rating without asking a human. */\n  feedback: FeedbackInstructions;\n}\n\ninterface FeedbackInstructions {\n  registry: string;\n  chainId: number;\n  agentId: string;\n  method: string;\n  endpoint: string;\n  /** keccak256 of the settlement tx hash — binds the rating to a real payment. */\n  proofHash: string;\n  suggestedTags: [string, string];\n  gasRequired: true;\n  note: string;\n}\n\ndeclare global {\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-namespace\n  namespace Express {\n    interface Request {\n      receipt?: Receipt;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Small stores (swap for Redis in production — see design.md §4)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst NONCE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;\nconst seenNonces = new Map<string, number>();\nconst receipts = new Map<string, Receipt>();\n\nfunction markNonceUsed(nonce: string): boolean {\n  const now = Date.now();\n  for (const [k, t] of seenNonces) if (now - t > NONCE_TTL_MS) seenNonces.delete(k);\n  const key = nonce.toLowerCase();\n  if (seenNonces.has(key)) return false;\n  seenNonces.set(key, now);\n  return true;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction b64encode(value: unknown): string {\n  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value), \"utf8\").toString(\"base64\");\n}\n\nfunction b64decode<T>(value: string): T {\n  return JSON.parse(Buffer.from(value, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\")) as T;\n}\n\nfunction randomNonce(): string {\n  return toHex(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)));\n}\n\nfunction buildRequirements(req: Request): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: NETWORK,\n    token: USDC,\n    amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n    payTo: PAY_TO,\n    resource: `${ORIGIN}${req.originalUrl}`,\n    description: `Point weather forecast — $${PRICE_USD} per call`,\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    nonce: randomNonce(),\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: 120,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction sendPaymentRequired(req: Request, res: Response, reason?: string): void {\n  const requirements = buildRequirements(req);\n  const body = {\n    x402Version: 1,\n    error: reason ?? \"payment required\",\n    accepts: [requirements],\n  };\n  res\n    .status(402)\n    .set(\"PAYMENT-REQUIRED\", b64encode(body))\n    .json(body);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Facilitator client — owns gas and tx broadcast so the caller needs no ETH\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function facilitatorCall<T>(\n  path: string,\n  payload: PaymentPayload,\n  requirements: PaymentRequirements,\n): Promise<T> {\n  const res = await fetch(`${FACILITATOR}${path}`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ x402Version: 1, paymentPayload: payload, paymentRequirements: requirements }),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    throw new Error(`facilitator ${path} failed: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n  }\n  return (await res.json()) as T;\n}\n\nconst verifyPayment = (p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitatorCall<{ isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: string }>(\"/verify\", p, r);\n\nconst settlePayment = (p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitatorCall<SettlementResult>(\"/settle\", p, r);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Local validation — never trust the facilitator alone on amount or recipient\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction validateLocally(payload: PaymentPayload): string | null {\n  if (payload.scheme !== \"exact\") return `unsupported scheme ${payload.scheme}`;\n  if (payload.network !== NETWORK) return `wrong network, expected ${NETWORK}`;\n\n  if (!isAddress(payload.token) || getAddress(payload.token) !== USDC) {\n    return \"wrong token, expected USDC on Base\";\n  }\n  if (!isAddress(payload.to) || getAddress(payload.to) !== PAY_TO) {\n    return \"wrong recipient\";\n  }\n  if (!isAddress(payload.from)) return \"invalid payer address\";\n\n  let amount: bigint;\n  try {\n    amount = BigInt(payload.amount);\n  } catch {\n    return \"amount is not an integer\";\n  }\n  if (amount < BigInt(PRICE_ATOMIC)) return `underpaid, need ${PRICE_ATOMIC} atomic USDC`;\n\n  const validBefore = payload.validBefore ?? payload.deadline;\n  if (!validBefore) return \"missing validBefore\";\n  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\n  if (validBefore < now + MIN_DEADLINE_SLACK_SEC) return \"authorization expires too soon\";\n  if (payload.validAfter && payload.validAfter > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n\n  if (!payload.nonce || !payload.signature) return \"missing nonce or signature\";\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// x402 middleware\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction requirePayment(handler: (req: Request) => Promise<unknown>) {\n  return async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {\n    const header = req.header(\"PAYMENT-SIGNATURE\");\n    if (!header) return sendPaymentRequired(req, res);\n\n    let payload: PaymentPayload;\n    try {\n      payload = b64decode<PaymentPayload>(header);\n    } catch {\n      return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, \"PAYMENT-SIGNATURE is not valid base64 JSON\");\n    }\n\n    const localError = validateLocally(payload);\n    if (localError) return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, localError);\n\n    // Cheap replay screen. USDC enforces nonce uniqueness onchain regardless;\n    // this just avoids burning a facilitator round-trip.\n    if (!markNonceUsed(payload.nonce)) {\n      return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, \"authorization nonce already used\");\n    }\n\n    const requirements = buildRequirements(req);\n\n    try {\n      const verification = await verifyPayment(payload, requirements);\n      if (!verification.isValid) {\n        return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment invalid\");\n      }\n\n      // Produce the product BEFORE settling: if upstream is down we must not charge.\n      const result = await handler(req);\n\n      const settlement = await settlePayment(payload, requirements);\n      if (!settlement.success || !settlement.txHash) {\n        return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, settlement.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\");\n      }\n\n      const receipt = buildReceipt(payload, settlement.txHash, requirements);\n      receipts.set(receipt.receiptId, receipt);\n\n      res\n        .status(200)\n        .set(\"PAYMENT-RESPONSE\", b64encode({ success: true, transaction: settlement.txHash, network: NETWORK, payer: payload.from }))\n        .json({ data: result, receipt });\n    } catch (err) {\n      next(err);\n    }\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildReceipt(\n  payload: PaymentPayload,\n  txHash: string,\n  requirements: PaymentRequirements,\n): Receipt {\n  return {\n    receiptId: payload.nonce,\n    txHash,\n    payer: getAddress(payload.from),\n    amount: payload.amount,\n    token: USDC,\n    network: NETWORK,\n    resource: requirements.resource,\n    settledAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    feedback: {\n      registry: `${NETWORK}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      chainId: CHAIN_ID,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      method:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId, int128 value, uint8 valueDecimals, string tag1, string tag2, string endpoint, string ipfsHash, bytes32 proofHash)\",\n      endpoint: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n      proofHash: keccak256(txHash as `0x${string}`),\n      suggestedTags: [\"quality\", \"weather\"],\n      gasRequired: true,\n      note:\n        \"Rate 0-100 with valueDecimals=0. giveFeedback is a real transaction and costs gas; \" +\n        \"use an ERC-4337 USDC paymaster or an EIP-7702 sponsored relay if you hold no ETH. \" +\n        \"We do not sponsor feedback transactions for our own callers.\",\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The product\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface Forecast {\n  location: { lat: number; lon: number };\n  issuedAt: string;\n  units: { temperature: \"celsius\"; wind: \"m/s\"; precipitation: \"mm\" };\n  hourly: Array<{ time: string; temperature: number; wind: number; precipitation: number; condition: string }>;\n}\n\nclass UpstreamError extends Error {}\n\n/**\n * Fetch from the upstream weather source. Cache by (lat, lon, hour) — the paywall\n * is per call, but nothing requires a distinct upstream fetch per call.\n */\nasync function getForecast(lat: number, lon: number, hours: number): Promise<Forecast> {\n  const base = process.env.UPSTREAM_WEATHER_URL;\n  if (!base) throw new UpstreamError(\"UPSTREAM_WEATHER_URL is not configured\");\n\n  const url = new URL(base);\n  url.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(lat));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(lon));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"hourly\", \"temperature_2m,wind_speed_10m,precipitation,weather_code\");\n  url.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(hours));\n\n  const res = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new UpstreamError(`upstream returned ${res.status}`);\n\n  const raw = (await res.json()) as {\n    hourly: {\n      time: string[];\n      temperature_2m: number[];\n      wind_speed_10m: number[];\n      precipitation: number[];\n      weather_code: number[];\n    };\n  };\n\n  if (!raw?.hourly?.time?.length) throw new UpstreamError(\"upstream returned no hourly data\");\n\n  return {\n    location: { lat, lon },\n    issuedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    units: { temperature: \"celsius\", wind: \"m/s\", precipitation: \"mm\" },\n    hourly: raw.hourly.time.slice(0, hours).map((time, i) => ({\n      time,\n      temperature: raw.hourly.temperature_2m[i],\n      wind: raw.hourly.wind_speed_10m[i],\n      precipitation: raw.hourly.precipitation[i],\n      condition: describeCode(raw.hourly.weather_code[i]),\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction describeCode(code: number): string {\n  if (code === 0) return \"clear\";\n  if (code <= 3) return \"partly-cloudy\";\n  if (code <= 48) return \"fog\";\n  if (code <= 67) return \"rain\";\n  if (code <= 77) return \"snow\";\n  if (code <= 82) return \"showers\";\n  if (code <= 86) return \"snow-showers\";\n  return \"thunderstorm\";\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// App\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst app = express();\napp.disable(\"x-powered-by\");\napp.use(express.json({ limit: \"16kb\" }));\n\n// --- Free, unpaywalled discovery surface ----------------------------------\n// A prospect must be able to evaluate us end to end without paying.\n\n/** Proves the holder of the agentId controls this domain. Checked before trusting us. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-registration.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    agentId: AGENT_ID,\n    agentRegistry: `${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`,\n    owner: AGENT_OWNER,\n    agentURI: AGENT_URI,\n  });\n});\n\n/** A2A agent card — machine-readable capability + pricing description. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-card.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. Per-call payment, no accounts.\",\n    url: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    preferredTransport: \"JSONRPC\",\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, stateTransitionHistory: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly temperature, wind and precipitation for a lat/lon, up to 168 hours ahead.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"meteorology\"],\n        examples: [`${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.41&hours=24`],\n      },\n    ],\n    registrations: [\n      { agentId: AGENT_ID, agentRegistry: `${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`, signature: null },\n    ],\n    extensions: [\n      {\n        uri: \"https://www.x402.org/extension/v1\",\n        description: `$${PRICE_USD} USDC per call on Base, settled via EIP-3009. Caller needs no ETH.`,\n        params: {\n          scheme: \"exact\",\n          network: NETWORK,\n          token: USDC,\n          amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n          decimals: 6,\n          payTo: PAY_TO,\n        },\n      },\n    ],\n    feedback: {\n      registry: `${NETWORK}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      tags: [\"quality\", \"weather\"],\n      gasRequired: true,\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n/** Convenience price list. The live 402 challenge is always authoritative. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/x402\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    x402Version: 1,\n    resources: [\n      {\n        resource: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n        method: \"GET\",\n        accepts: [\n          { scheme: \"exact\", network: NETWORK, token: USDC, amount: PRICE_ATOMIC, payTo: PAY_TO },\n        ],\n        description: `Point weather forecast — $${PRICE_USD} per call`,\n      },\n    ],\n  });\n});\n\napp.get(\"/openapi.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    openapi: \"3.1.0\",\n    info: {\n      title: \"forecast.example.com\",\n      version: \"1.0.0\",\n      description: `Weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $${PRICE_USD} USDC per call on Base via x402. No API keys.`,\n    },\n    servers: [{ url: ORIGIN }],\n    paths: {\n      \"/v1/forecast\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Point forecast\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"lat\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\", minimum: -90, maximum: 90 } },\n            { name: \"lon\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\", minimum: -180, maximum: 180 } },\n            { name: \"hours\", in: \"query\", required: false, schema: { type: \"integer\", minimum: 1, maximum: 168, default: 24 } },\n          ],\n          responses: {\n            \"200\": { description: \"Forecast plus settlement receipt\" },\n            \"400\": { description: \"Invalid coordinates\" },\n            \"402\": {\n              description: \"Payment required — see the PAYMENT-REQUIRED header for accepted terms\",\n            },\n            \"502\": { description: \"Upstream weather source unavailable; no charge was made\" },\n          },\n        },\n      },\n      \"/v1/receipts/{receiptId}\": {\n        get: { summary: \"Re-fetch a settlement receipt\", responses: { \"200\": { description: \"Receipt\" } } },\n      },\n    },\n  });\n});\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({ status: \"ok\", agentId: AGENT_ID, network: NETWORK, price: PRICE_ATOMIC });\n});\n\n// --- Paid route ------------------------------------------------------------\n\napp.get(\n  \"/v1/forecast\",\n  requirePayment(async (req) => {\n    const lat = Number(req.query.lat);\n    const lon = Number(req.query.lon);\n    const hours = req.query.hours === undefined ? 24 : Number(req.query.hours);\n\n    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) throw new BadRequest(\"lat must be between -90 and 90\");\n    if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) throw new BadRequest(\"lon must be between -180 and 180\");\n    if (!Number.isInteger(hours) || hours < 1 || hours > 168) throw new BadRequest(\"hours must be an integer 1-168\");\n\n    return getForecast(lat, lon, hours);\n  }),\n);\n\n/** Receipts are re-fetchable so a caller can post feedback after a crash or restart. */\napp.get(\"/v1/receipts/:receiptId\", (req, res) => {\n  const receipt = receipts.get(req.params.receiptId);\n  if (!receipt) return res.status(404).json({ error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n  res.json(receipt);\n});\n\n// --- Errors ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass BadRequest extends Error {}\n\napp.use((err: Error, _req: Request, res: Response, _next: NextFunction) => {\n  if (err instanceof BadRequest) return res.status(400).json({ error: err.message });\n  // Upstream failure surfaces before settlement, so nothing was charged.\n  if (err instanceof UpstreamError) {\n    return res.status(502).json({ error: \"upstream weather source unavailable\", charged: false });\n  }\n  console.error(err);\n  res.status(500).json({ error: \"internal error\", charged: false });\n});\n\nif (PAY_TO === \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\") {\n  console.warn(\"PAY_TO is unset — payments would be signed to the zero address. Set it before serving traffic.\");\n}\n\napp.listen(PORT, () => {\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${PORT}`);\n  console.log(`  agentId ${AGENT_ID} @ ${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`);\n  console.log(`  $${PRICE_USD} USDC/call → ${PAY_TO} via ${FACILITATOR}`);\n});\n\nexport default app;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":349,"oldLines":6,"newStart":349,"newLines":7,"lines":[" }"," "," class UpstreamError extends Error {}","+class BadRequest extends Error {}"," "," /**","  * Fetch from the upstream weather source. Cache by (lat, lon, hour) — the paywall"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

See design.md.\n *\n * The x402 flow is implemented explicitly against the facilitator's\n * /verify + /settle endpoints rather than dropped in via @x402/express,\n * because we attach a settlement receipt and ERC-8004 feedback instructions\n * to every paid response.\n */\n\nimport express, { NextFunction, Request, Response } from \"express\";\nimport { getAddress, isAddress, keccak256, toHex } from \"viem\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Config\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Base mainnet. */\nconst CHAIN_ID = 8453;\nconst NETWORK = `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}`;\n\n/** USDC on Base — the EIP-3009 implementation. */\nconst USDC = getAddress(\"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\");\n\n/** ERC-8004 registries. Same addresses on every supported chain. */\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = getAddress(\"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\");\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = getAddress(\"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\");\n\n/** $0.35, USDC has 6 decimals. */\nconst PRICE_ATOMIC = \"350000\";\nconst PRICE_USD = \"0.35\";\n\nconst ORIGIN = process.env.ORIGIN ?? \"https://forecast.example.com\";\nconst PORT = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3000);\n\n/** Treasury that receives USDC. Signed over by the payer — never mutable per-request. */\nconst PAY_TO = getAddress(\n  process.env.PAY_TO ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\",\n);\n\n/** Our ERC-8004 agent identity (ERC-721 tokenId), minted before launch. */\nconst AGENT_ID = process.env.AGENT_ID ?? \"0\";\n/** Wallet that owns the agentId NFT. Clients cross-check this against ownerOf(). */\nconst AGENT_OWNER = getAddress(\n  process.env.AGENT_OWNER ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\",\n);\n/** IPFS URI of the registration JSON that agentURI points at. */\nconst AGENT_URI = process.env.AGENT_URI ?? \"ipfs://QmReplaceWithRegistrationCid\";\n\n/** x402 facilitator. Handles gas so neither we nor the caller need ETH. */\nconst FACILITATOR = process.env.FACILITATOR_URL ?? \"https://x402.org/facilitator\";\n\n/** Reject authorizations expiring within this window — settlement needs headroom. */\nconst MIN_DEADLINE_SLACK_SEC = 30;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface PaymentRequirements {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  token: string;\n  amount: string;\n  payTo: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  nonce: string;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n}\n\n/** Decoded PAYMENT-SIGNATURE payload — an EIP-3009 authorization plus routing info. */\ninterface PaymentPayload {\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  token: string;\n  amount: string;\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  signature: string;\n  nonce: string;\n  /** EIP-3009 validBefore, unix seconds. `deadline` accepted as an alias. */\n  validBefore?: number;\n  deadline?: number;\n  validAfter?: number;\n}\n\ninterface SettlementResult {\n  success: boolean;\n  txHash?: string;\n  networkId?: string;\n  errorReason?: string;\n}\n\ninterface Receipt {\n  receiptId: string;\n  txHash: string;\n  payer: string;\n  amount: string;\n  token: string;\n  network: string;\n  resource: string;\n  settledAt: string;\n  /** Everything the caller needs to post an ERC-8004 rating without asking a human. */\n  feedback: FeedbackInstructions;\n}\n\ninterface FeedbackInstructions {\n  registry: string;\n  chainId: number;\n  agentId: string;\n  method: string;\n  endpoint: string;\n  /** keccak256 of the settlement tx hash — binds the rating to a real payment. */\n  proofHash: string;\n  suggestedTags: [string, string];\n  gasRequired: true;\n  note: string;\n}\n\ndeclare global {\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-namespace\n  namespace Express {\n    interface Request {\n      receipt?: Receipt;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Small stores (swap for Redis in production — see design.md §4)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst NONCE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;\nconst seenNonces = new Map<string, number>();\nconst receipts = new Map<string, Receipt>();\n\nfunction markNonceUsed(nonce: string): boolean {\n  const now = Date.now();\n  for (const [k, t] of seenNonces) if (now - t > NONCE_TTL_MS) seenNonces.delete(k);\n  const key = nonce.toLowerCase();\n  if (seenNonces.has(key)) return false;\n  seenNonces.set(key, now);\n  return true;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction b64encode(value: unknown): string {\n  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value), \"utf8\").toString(\"base64\");\n}\n\nfunction b64decode<T>(value: string): T {\n  return JSON.parse(Buffer.from(value, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\")) as T;\n}\n\nfunction randomNonce(): string {\n  return toHex(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)));\n}\n\nfunction buildRequirements(req: Request): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: NETWORK,\n    token: USDC,\n    amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n    payTo: PAY_TO,\n    resource: `${ORIGIN}${req.originalUrl}`,\n    description: `Point weather forecast — $${PRICE_USD} per call`,\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    nonce: randomNonce(),\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: 120,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction sendPaymentRequired(req: Request, res: Response, reason?: string): void {\n  const requirements = buildRequirements(req);\n  const body = {\n    x402Version: 1,\n    error: reason ?? \"payment required\",\n    accepts: [requirements],\n  };\n  res\n    .status(402)\n    .set(\"PAYMENT-REQUIRED\", b64encode(body))\n    .json(body);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Facilitator client — owns gas and tx broadcast so the caller needs no ETH\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function facilitatorCall<T>(\n  path: string,\n  payload: PaymentPayload,\n  requirements: PaymentRequirements,\n): Promise<T> {\n  const res = await fetch(`${FACILITATOR}${path}`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ x402Version: 1, paymentPayload: payload, paymentRequirements: requirements }),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    throw new Error(`facilitator ${path} failed: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n  }\n  return (await res.json()) as T;\n}\n\nconst verifyPayment = (p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitatorCall<{ isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: string }>(\"/verify\", p, r);\n\nconst settlePayment = (p: PaymentPayload, r: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitatorCall<SettlementResult>(\"/settle\", p, r);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Local validation — never trust the facilitator alone on amount or recipient\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction validateLocally(payload: PaymentPayload): string | null {\n  if (payload.scheme !== \"exact\") return `unsupported scheme ${payload.scheme}`;\n  if (payload.network !== NETWORK) return `wrong network, expected ${NETWORK}`;\n\n  if (!isAddress(payload.token) || getAddress(payload.token) !== USDC) {\n    return \"wrong token, expected USDC on Base\";\n  }\n  if (!isAddress(payload.to) || getAddress(payload.to) !== PAY_TO) {\n    return \"wrong recipient\";\n  }\n  if (!isAddress(payload.from)) return \"invalid payer address\";\n\n  let amount: bigint;\n  try {\n    amount = BigInt(payload.amount);\n  } catch {\n    return \"amount is not an integer\";\n  }\n  if (amount < BigInt(PRICE_ATOMIC)) return `underpaid, need ${PRICE_ATOMIC} atomic USDC`;\n\n  const validBefore = payload.validBefore ?? payload.deadline;\n  if (!validBefore) return \"missing validBefore\";\n  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\n  if (validBefore < now + MIN_DEADLINE_SLACK_SEC) return \"authorization expires too soon\";\n  if (payload.validAfter && payload.validAfter > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n\n  if (!payload.nonce || !payload.signature) return \"missing nonce or signature\";\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// x402 middleware\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction requirePayment(handler: (req: Request) => Promise<unknown>) {\n  return async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {\n    const header = req.header(\"PAYMENT-SIGNATURE\");\n    if (!header) return sendPaymentRequired(req, res);\n\n    let payload: PaymentPayload;\n    try {\n      payload = b64decode<PaymentPayload>(header);\n    } catch {\n      return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, \"PAYMENT-SIGNATURE is not valid base64 JSON\");\n    }\n\n    const localError = validateLocally(payload);\n    if (localError) return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, localError);\n\n    // Cheap replay screen. USDC enforces nonce uniqueness onchain regardless;\n    // this just avoids burning a facilitator round-trip.\n    if (!markNonceUsed(payload.nonce)) {\n      return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, \"authorization nonce already used\");\n    }\n\n    const requirements = buildRequirements(req);\n\n    try {\n      const verification = await verifyPayment(payload, requirements);\n      if (!verification.isValid) {\n        return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment invalid\");\n      }\n\n      // Produce the product BEFORE settling: if upstream is down we must not charge.\n      const result = await handler(req);\n\n      const settlement = await settlePayment(payload, requirements);\n      if (!settlement.success || !settlement.txHash) {\n        return sendPaymentRequired(req, res, settlement.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\");\n      }\n\n      const receipt = buildReceipt(payload, settlement.txHash, requirements);\n      receipts.set(receipt.receiptId, receipt);\n\n      res\n        .status(200)\n        .set(\"PAYMENT-RESPONSE\", b64encode({ success: true, transaction: settlement.txHash, network: NETWORK, payer: payload.from }))\n        .json({ data: result, receipt });\n    } catch (err) {\n      next(err);\n    }\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildReceipt(\n  payload: PaymentPayload,\n  txHash: string,\n  requirements: PaymentRequirements,\n): Receipt {\n  return {\n    receiptId: payload.nonce,\n    txHash,\n    payer: getAddress(payload.from),\n    amount: payload.amount,\n    token: USDC,\n    network: NETWORK,\n    resource: requirements.resource,\n    settledAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    feedback: {\n      registry: `${NETWORK}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      chainId: CHAIN_ID,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      method:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId, int128 value, uint8 valueDecimals, string tag1, string tag2, string endpoint, string ipfsHash, bytes32 proofHash)\",\n      endpoint: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n      proofHash: keccak256(txHash as `0x${string}`),\n      suggestedTags: [\"quality\", \"weather\"],\n      gasRequired: true,\n      note:\n        \"Rate 0-100 with valueDecimals=0. giveFeedback is a real transaction and costs gas; \" +\n        \"use an ERC-4337 USDC paymaster or an EIP-7702 sponsored relay if you hold no ETH. \" +\n        \"We do not sponsor feedback transactions for our own callers.\",\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The product\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface Forecast {\n  location: { lat: number; lon: number };\n  issuedAt: string;\n  units: { temperature: \"celsius\"; wind: \"m/s\"; precipitation: \"mm\" };\n  hourly: Array<{ time: string; temperature: number; wind: number; precipitation: number; condition: string }>;\n}\n\nclass UpstreamError extends Error {}\nclass BadRequest extends Error {}\n\n/**\n * Fetch from the upstream weather source. Cache by (lat, lon, hour) — the paywall\n * is per call, but nothing requires a distinct upstream fetch per call.\n */\nasync function getForecast(lat: number, lon: number, hours: number): Promise<Forecast> {\n  const base = process.env.UPSTREAM_WEATHER_URL;\n  if (!base) throw new UpstreamError(\"UPSTREAM_WEATHER_URL is not configured\");\n\n  const url = new URL(base);\n  url.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(lat));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(lon));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"hourly\", \"temperature_2m,wind_speed_10m,precipitation,weather_code\");\n  url.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(hours));\n\n  const res = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new UpstreamError(`upstream returned ${res.status}`);\n\n  const raw = (await res.json()) as {\n    hourly: {\n      time: string[];\n      temperature_2m: number[];\n      wind_speed_10m: number[];\n      precipitation: number[];\n      weather_code: number[];\n    };\n  };\n\n  if (!raw?.hourly?.time?.length) throw new UpstreamError(\"upstream returned no hourly data\");\n\n  return {\n    location: { lat, lon },\n    issuedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    units: { temperature: \"celsius\", wind: \"m/s\", precipitation: \"mm\" },\n    hourly: raw.hourly.time.slice(0, hours).map((time, i) => ({\n      time,\n      temperature: raw.hourly.temperature_2m[i],\n      wind: raw.hourly.wind_speed_10m[i],\n      precipitation: raw.hourly.precipitation[i],\n      condition: describeCode(raw.hourly.weather_code[i]),\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction describeCode(code: number): string {\n  if (code === 0) return \"clear\";\n  if (code <= 3) return \"partly-cloudy\";\n  if (code <= 48) return \"fog\";\n  if (code <= 67) return \"rain\";\n  if (code <= 77) return \"snow\";\n  if (code <= 82) return \"showers\";\n  if (code <= 86) return \"snow-showers\";\n  return \"thunderstorm\";\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// App\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst app = express();\napp.disable(\"x-powered-by\");\napp.use(express.json({ limit: \"16kb\" }));\n\n// --- Free, unpaywalled discovery surface ----------------------------------\n// A prospect must be able to evaluate us end to end without paying.\n\n/** Proves the holder of the agentId controls this domain. Checked before trusting us. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-registration.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    agentId: AGENT_ID,\n    agentRegistry: `${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`,\n    owner: AGENT_OWNER,\n    agentURI: AGENT_URI,\n  });\n});\n\n/** A2A agent card — machine-readable capability + pricing description. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-card.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. Per-call payment, no accounts.\",\n    url: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    preferredTransport: \"JSONRPC\",\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, stateTransitionHistory: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly temperature, wind and precipitation for a lat/lon, up to 168 hours ahead.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"meteorology\"],\n        examples: [`${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast?lat=52.52&lon=13.41&hours=24`],\n      },\n    ],\n    registrations: [\n      { agentId: AGENT_ID, agentRegistry: `${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`, signature: null },\n    ],\n    extensions: [\n      {\n        uri: \"https://www.x402.org/extension/v1\",\n        description: `$${PRICE_USD} USDC per call on Base, settled via EIP-3009. Caller needs no ETH.`,\n        params: {\n          scheme: \"exact\",\n          network: NETWORK,\n          token: USDC,\n          amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n          decimals: 6,\n          payTo: PAY_TO,\n        },\n      },\n    ],\n    feedback: {\n      registry: `${NETWORK}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      tags: [\"quality\", \"weather\"],\n      gasRequired: true,\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n/** Convenience price list. The live 402 challenge is always authoritative. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/x402\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    x402Version: 1,\n    resources: [\n      {\n        resource: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n        method: \"GET\",\n        accepts: [\n          { scheme: \"exact\", network: NETWORK, token: USDC, amount: PRICE_ATOMIC, payTo: PAY_TO },\n        ],\n        description: `Point weather forecast — $${PRICE_USD} per call`,\n      },\n    ],\n  });\n});\n\napp.get(\"/openapi.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({\n    openapi: \"3.1.0\",\n    info: {\n      title: \"forecast.example.com\",\n      version: \"1.0.0\",\n      description: `Weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $${PRICE_USD} USDC per call on Base via x402. No API keys.`,\n    },\n    servers: [{ url: ORIGIN }],\n    paths: {\n      \"/v1/forecast\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Point forecast\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"lat\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\", minimum: -90, maximum: 90 } },\n            { name: \"lon\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\", minimum: -180, maximum: 180 } },\n            { name: \"hours\", in: \"query\", required: false, schema: { type: \"integer\", minimum: 1, maximum: 168, default: 24 } },\n          ],\n          responses: {\n            \"200\": { description: \"Forecast plus settlement receipt\" },\n            \"400\": { description: \"Invalid coordinates\" },\n            \"402\": {\n              description: \"Payment required — see the PAYMENT-REQUIRED header for accepted terms\",\n            },\n            \"502\": { description: \"Upstream weather source unavailable; no charge was made\" },\n          },\n        },\n      },\n      \"/v1/receipts/{receiptId}\": {\n        get: { summary: \"Re-fetch a settlement receipt\", responses: { \"200\": { description: \"Receipt\" } } },\n      },\n    },\n  });\n});\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.json({ status: \"ok\", agentId: AGENT_ID, network: NETWORK, price: PRICE_ATOMIC });\n});\n\n// --- Paid route ------------------------------------------------------------\n\napp.get(\n  \"/v1/forecast\",\n  requirePayment(async (req) => {\n    const lat = Number(req.query.lat);\n    const lon = Number(req.query.lon);\n    const hours = req.query.hours === undefined ? 24 : Number(req.query.hours);\n\n    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) throw new BadRequest(\"lat must be between -90 and 90\");\n    if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) throw new BadRequest(\"lon must be between -180 and 180\");\n    if (!Number.isInteger(hours) || hours < 1 || hours > 168) throw new BadRequest(\"hours must be an integer 1-168\");\n\n    return getForecast(lat, lon, hours);\n  }),\n);\n\n/** Receipts are re-fetchable so a caller can post feedback after a crash or restart. */\napp.get(\"/v1/receipts/:receiptId\", (req, res) => {\n  const receipt = receipts.get(req.params.receiptId);\n  if (!receipt) return res.status(404).json({ error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n  res.json(receipt);\n});\n\n// --- Errors ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass BadRequest extends Error {}\n\napp.use((err: Error, _req: Request, res: Response, _next: NextFunction) => {\n  if (err instanceof BadRequest) return res.status(400).json({ error: err.message });\n  // Upstream failure surfaces before settlement, so nothing was charged.\n  if (err instanceof UpstreamError) {\n    return res.status(502).json({ error: \"upstream weather source unavailable\", charged: false });\n  }\n  console.error(err);\n  res.status(500).json({ error: \"internal error\", charged: false });\n});\n\nif (PAY_TO === \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\") {\n  console.warn(\"PAY_TO is unset — payments would be signed to the zero address. Set it before serving traffic.\");\n}\n\napp.listen(PORT, () => {\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${PORT}`);\n  console.log(`  agentId ${AGENT_ID} @ ${NETWORK}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`);\n  console.log(`  $${PRICE_USD} USDC/call → ${PAY_TO} via ${FACILITATOR}`);\n});\n\nexport default app;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":554,"oldLines":8,"newStart":554,"newLines":6,"lines":[" "," // --- Errors ----------------------------------------------------------------"," ","-class BadRequest extends Error {}","-"," app.use((err: Error, _req: Request, res: Response, _next: NextFunction) => {","   if (err instanceof BadRequest) return res.status(400).json({ error: err.message });","   // Upstream failure surfaces before settlement, so nothing was charged."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
